### [71] [Dive into Claude Code: The Design Space of Today's and Future AI Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14228)
*Jiacheng Liu,Xiaohan Zhao,Xinyi Shang,Zhiqiang Shen*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文通过分析 Claude Code 的 TypeScript 源代码，并与 OpenClaw 比较，揭示了 AI 编码智能体的架构设计。研究识别出五个核心人类价值观、十三项设计原则，并详细分析了权限系统、上下文压缩、扩展机制等核心组件。研究表明，相同的 design questions 在不同部署上下文中会产生不同的 architectural answers。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解和揭示 Claude Code 这一智能编程工具的架构设计逻辑，识别其背后的设计决策和价值考量，并探讨不同部署上下文下架构选择的差异性。

Method: 通过分析公开可用的 TypeScript 源代码对 Claude Code 进行全面架构分析，并与 OpenClaw（一个独立的开源 AI 智能体系统）进行比较研究，采用设计推理方法。

Result: 识别出五个驱动架构的人类价值观（人类决策权、安全保障、可靠执行、能力增强、情境适应性），追溯了十三个设计原则到具体实现选择。核心是一个简单的 while 循环，代码主要集中在权限系统（7 种模式+ML 分类器）、五层压缩管道（上下文管理）、四种扩展机制（MCP/插件/skills/hooks）、子智能体委托机制和工作区隔离，以及追加导向的会话存储。

Conclusion: 研究最终确定了未来智能体系统的六个开放设计方向，基于实证、建筑和政策文献。核心发现是相同的 recurr设计问题在不同部署上下文下会产生不同的架构答案。

Abstract: Claude Code is an agentic coding tool that can run shell commands, edit files, and call external services on behalf of the user. This study describes its comprehensive architecture by analyzing the publicly available TypeScript source code and further comparing it with OpenClaw, an independent open-source AI agent system that answers many of the same design questions from a different deployment context. Our analysis identifies five human values, philosophies, and needs that motivate the architecture (human decision authority, safety and security, reliable execution, capability amplification, and contextual adaptability) and traces them through thirteen design principles to specific implementation choices. The core of the system is a simple while-loop that calls the model, runs tools, and repeats. Most of the code, however, lives in the systems around this loop: a permission system with seven modes and an ML-based classifier, a five-layer compaction pipeline for context management, four extensibility mechanisms (MCP, plugins, skills, and hooks), a subagent delegation mechanism with worktree isolation, and append-oriented session storage. A comparison with OpenClaw, a multi-channel personal assistant gateway, shows that the same recurring design questions produce different architectural answers when the deployment context changes: from per-action safety classification to perimeter-level access control, from a single CLI loop to an embedded runtime within a gateway control plane, and from context-window extensions to gateway-wide capability registration. We finally identify six open design directions for future agent systems, grounded in recent empirical, architectural, and policy literature.

</details>


### [72] [Graph-Based ECO and Patch Generation for High-Level Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14248)
*Alireza Azadi,Paul Rigge,Ethan Mahintorabi,Kenneth B. Kent*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文针对高层次综合（HLS）工具中工程变更订单（ECO）支持不足的问题，提出了一种面向Google XLS的基于图的ECO方法。该方法利用图编辑距离（GED）算法检测原始与修改后的中间表示（IR）之间的结构差异，并将其转换为补丁操作，同时保持语义正确性和流水线寄存器调度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前HLS工具对工程变更订单（ECO）的支持非常有限，导致设计后期修改成本高昂且效率低下。这一问题阻碍了HLS在实际生产环境中的灵活应用。

Method: 1. 使用图编辑距离（GED）算法检测原始IR与修改后IR之间的结构差异；2. 将结构差异转换为补丁操作（patch operations）；3. 开发补丁应用机制以强制执行XLS IR约束并保持语义正确性；4. 设计调度约束方案以维持原始流水线寄存器。

Result: 实验结果表明：该方法在多个XLS设计上实现了高结构重用率、有效保持调度约束，并达到完全功能正确性。

Conclusion: 该方法对于生产级HLS流程具有实用价值，可有效支持设计后期的增量修改，降低变更成本。

Abstract: High-level synthesis (HLS) tools offer limited support for Engineering Change Orders (ECOs), making late-stage design modifications challenging and costly. This paper introduces a graph-based ECO methodology tailored for Google XLS. A Graph Edit Distance (GED) algorithm is used to detect structural differences between original and revised intermediate representations (IRs), which are then transformed into patch operations. A patch application mechanism is developed to enforce XLS IR constraints while preserving semantic correctness, together with a schedule constraining scheme that maintains the original pipeline registers. Experiments across several XLS designs demonstrate high structural reuse ratios, effective schedule preservation, and full functional correctness, highlighting the practicality of the approach for production HLS flows.

</details>


### [73] [LLMs taking shortcuts in test generation: A study with SAP HANA and LevelDB](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14437)
*Vekil Bekmyradov,Noah C. Pütz,Thomas Bartz-Beielstein*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文通过在开源系统(LevelDB)与商业闭源系统(SAP HANA)上的自动化测试生成任务，对LLM的推理能力进行实证研究，发现LLM在公开基准测试上表现优异但面对未见过的复杂领域时表现不佳，表明其依赖浅层启发式方法而非真正的推理能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 验证LLM是否真正具备推理能力，还是仅依赖表面模式和记忆。通过使用训练数据中必然不存在的商业闭源代码(SAP HANA)作为测试集，结合认知科学中的评估原则，解决公开基准测试可能存在的数据泄露问题。

Method: 结合Mitchell的机制导向评估方法论与经验软件测试方法，使用变异分数(mutation score)和迭代编译器反馈修复循环来同时评估准确性和底层推理策略。

Result: LLM在熟悉的开源基准测试上表现出色，但在未见过的复杂领域(SAP HANA)上表现挣扎，且往往优先追求可编译性而非语义有效性。

Conclusion: 当前LLM缺乏稳健的推理能力，主要依赖浅层捷径而非真正的泛化能力，需要构建能够惩罚trivial shortcuts、奖励真正泛化的评估框架。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have achieved impressive results on public benchmarks, often leading to claims of advanced reasoning and understanding. However, recent research in cognitive science reveals that these models sometimes rely on shallow heuristics and memorization, taking shortcuts rather than demonstrating genuine cognitive abilities. This paper investigates LLM behavior in automated test generation for software, contrasting performance on an open-source system (LevelDB) with SAP HANA, one of the most widely deployed commercial database systems worldwide, whose proprietary codebase is guaranteed to be absent from training data. We combine cognitive evaluation principles, drawing on Mitchell's mechanism-focused assessment methodology, with empirical software testing, employing mutation score and iterative compiler-feedback repair loops to assess both accuracy and underlying reasoning strategies. Results show that LLMs excel on familiar, open-source benchmarks but struggle with unseen, complex domains, often prioritizing compilability over semantic effectiveness. These findings provide independent software engineering evidence for the broader claim that current LLMs lack robust reasoning, and highlight the need for evaluation frameworks that penalize trivial shortcuts and reward true generalization.

</details>


### [74] [Asking What Matters: Reward-Driven Clarification for Software Engineering Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14624)
*Sanidhya Vijayvargiya,Vijay Viswanathan,Graham Neubig*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文研究软件工程任务中的有效澄清问题策略，通过Shapley归因和分布比较，识别出影响任务成功的关键信息类型，并提出基于任务相关性和用户可回答性两个属性的多阶段强化学习方法训练CLARITI模型，在匹配GPT-5分辨率的同时减少41%的问题生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类在指定任务时往往不完整，但助手必须知道何时以及如何提出澄清问题。有效的澄清在软件工程中具有挑战性，因为并非所有缺失信息都同等重要，且问题必须针对用户实际能够提供的信息。

Method: 使用Shapley归因和分布比较来量化不同类型信息对任务成功的影响；将任务相关性（预测成功的信息）和用户可回答性（用户实际能提供什么）作为多阶段强化学习的奖励函数，训练8B参数的CLARITI澄清模块。

Result: CLARITI在未指定问题上的分辨率与GPT-5相当，但问题生成数量减少41%。

Conclusion: 将奖励设计建立在信息影响和用户可回答性的实证分析基础上，可以提高澄清效率。

Abstract: Humans often specify tasks incompletely, so assistants must know when and how to ask clarifying questions. However, effective clarification remains challenging in software engineering tasks as not all missing information is equally valuable, and questions must target information users can realistically provide. We study clarification in real software engineering tasks by quantifying which types of information most affect task success and which questions elicit useful responses from simulated users. Using Shapley attribution and distributional comparisons, we identify two key properties of effective clarification: task relevance (which information predicts success) and user answerability (what users can realistically provide). We operationalize these properties as multi-stage reinforcement learning rewards to train CLARITI, an 8B-parameter clarification module, that matches GPT-5's resolution rate on underspecified issues while generating 41% fewer questions. Our results suggest that grounding reward design in empirical analysis of information impact and user answerability improves clarification efficiency.

</details>


### [75] [AIPC: Agent-Based Automation for AI Model Deployment with Qualcomm AI Runtime](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14661)
*Jianhao Su,Zhanwei Wu,ShengTing Huang,Weidong Feng*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: AIPC是一个基于AI智能体的边缘AI模型部署自动化框架，通过标准化部署阶段、领域知识注入和阶段验证机制，将原本需要专家知识的复杂部署流程压缩至数分钟完成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 边缘AI模型部署涉及模型转换、算子兼容性处理、量化校准、运行时集成和精度验证等多个阶段，流程长、易失败，严重依赖部署专业知识，尤其在针对硬件特定推理运行时场景下。

Method: 通过将部署流程分解为标准化、可验证的阶段，并注入部署领域知识（Agent Skills、辅助脚本、阶段验证循环）；在Qualcomm AI Runtime（QAIRT）上测试视觉、多模态和语音模型。

Result: 对于结构规则的视觉模型，AIPC可在7-20分钟内完成PyTorch到QNN/SNPE可运行推理，API成本约0.7-10美元；对于复杂模型（算子支持有限、动态形状、自回归解码结构等），仍需进一步改进。

Conclusion: AIPC显著降低了AI模型硬件部署的专业门槛和时间成本，使原本需要专业知识的复杂部署流程实现自动化，在7-20分钟内完成典型视觉模型的部署。

Abstract: Edge AI model deployment is a multi-stage engineering process involving model conversion, operator compatibility handling, quantization calibration, runtime integration, and accuracy validation. In
  practice, this workflow is long, failure-prone, and heavily dependent on deployment expertise, particularly when targeting hardware-specific inference runtimes. This technical report presents AIPC (AI
  Porting Conversion), an AI agent-driven approach for constrained automation of AI model deployment. AIPC decomposes deployment into standardized, verifiable stages and injects deployment-domain knowledge
  into agent execution through Agent Skills, helper scripts, and a stage-wise validation loop. This design reduces both the expertise barrier and the engineering time required for hardware deployment.
  Using Qualcomm AI Runtime (QAIRT) as the primary scenario, this report examines automated deployment across representative vision, multimodal, and speech models. In the cases covered here, AIPC can
  complete deployment from PyTorch to runnable QNN/SNPE inference within 7-20 minutes for structurally regular vision models, with indicative API costs roughly in the range of USD 0.7-10. For more complex
  models involving less-supported operators, dynamic shapes, or autoregressive decoding structures, fully automated deployment may still require further advances, but AIPC already provides practical support
  for execution, failure localization, and bounded repair.

</details>


### [76] [Bounded Autonomy for Enterprise AI: Typed Action Contracts and Consumer-Side Execution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14723)
*Sarmad Sohail,Ghufran Haider*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文针对大型语言模型作为企业软件自然语言接口时的安全问题，提出了一种"有界自主权"(bounded-autonomy)架构。该架构通过类型化操作契约、权限感知的能力暴露、作用域上下文、执行前验证、消费者侧执行边界和可选人工审批等多层安全机制，在25个场景测试中实现了23/25任务完成且零不安全执行，显著优于无约束AI配置(17/25)。研究表明移除安全层反而降低了系统可用性，结构化验证反馈能引导模型纠正错误。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型直接作为企业软件系统操作者存在安全隐患：模型错误会传播为未授权操作、格式错误的请求、跨工作区执行等代价高昂的故障。作者认为这本质上是一个执行架构问题，而非模型能力问题。

Method: 提出有界自主权架构，核心设计包括：(1)类型化操作契约约束可执行行为；(2)权限感知的能力暴露机制；(3)作用域限定的上下文；(4)副作用前验证；(5)消费者侧执行边界；(6)可选人工审批。企业应用保持业务逻辑和授权的权威来源，编排引擎基于显式发布操作清单运行。

Result: 在部署的多租户企业应用中评估三种条件：手动操作、安全层禁用的无约束AI、完全有界自主权。结果：完全有界自主权完成23/25任务，零不安全执行；无约束配置仅完成17/25。两类错误实体变更逃脱了消费者贡献层的所有防护。两种AI条件均实现比手动操作快13-18倍的速度提升。

Conclusion: 该有界自主权架构是使不完善的语言模型在企业系统中可运营化使用的可行方案。关键发现：移除安全层会降低系统实用性，结构化验证反馈能引导模型在更少轮次内达成正确结果；多项安全属性通过代码结构强制执行，无论模型输出如何均能拦截目标违规行为。

Abstract: Large language models are increasingly used as natural-language interfaces to enterprise software, but their direct use as system operators remains unsafe. Model errors can propagate into unauthorized actions, malformed requests, cross-workspace execution, and other costly failures. We argue this is primarily an execution architecture problem. We present a bounded-autonomy architecture in which language models may interpret intent and propose actions, but all executable behavior is constrained by typed action contracts, permission-aware capability exposure, scoped context, validation before side effects, consumer-side execution boundaries, and optional human approval. The enterprise application remains the source of truth for business logic and authorization, while the orchestration engine operates over an explicit published actions manifest. We evaluate the architecture in a deployed multi-tenant enterprise application across three conditions: manual operation, unconstrained AI with safety layers disabled, and full bounded autonomy. Across 25 scenario trials spanning seven failure families, the bounded-autonomy system completed 23 of 25 tasks with zero unsafe executions, while the unconstrained configuration completed only 17 of 25. Two wrong-entity mutations escaped all consumer-contributed layers; only disambiguation and confirmation mechanisms intercept this class. Both AI conditions delivered 13-18x speedup over manual operation. Critically, removing safety layers made the system less useful: structured validation feedback guided the model to correct outcomes in fewer turns, while the unconstrained system hallucinated success. Several safety properties are structurally enforced by code and intercepted all targeted violations regardless of model output. The result is a practical, deployed architecture for making imperfect language models operationally useful in enterprise systems.

</details>


### [77] [SWE-TRACE: Optimizing Long-Horizon SWE Agents Through Rubric Process Reward Models and Heuristic Test-Time Scaling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14820)
*Hao Han,Jin Xie,Xuehao Ma,Weiquan Zhu,Ziyao Zhang,ZhiLiang Long,Hongkai Chen,Qingwen Ye*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: SWE-TRACE是一个统一优化SWE智能体生命周期的框架，通过数据筛选、RL训练和测试时推理三阶段协同，在保持高解决率的同时大幅降低token消耗和推理延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前SWE智能体受限于三个核心瓶颈：(1) 示范数据未优化；(2) 稀疏执行奖励导致RL训练不稳定；(3) 推理时扩展计算成本过高。这些问题引发token膨胀、奖励黑客和策略退化。

Method: 提出三阶段统一方案：(1) 基于LLM多任务级联与逐步oracle验证，从60K样本中筛选出token高效的最短路径轨迹用于SFT；(2) 设计Memory-Augmented Agentic RL pipeline，引入Rubric-Based PRM和辅助Rubric-Agent提供细粒度过程反馈；(3) 在测试时将PRM重用于启发式搜索，通过动态评估和剪枝候选动作避免并行采样的延迟开销。

Result: 在标准SWE基准上达到SOTA，显著提升问题解决率，同时大幅降低token消耗和推理延迟。

Conclusion: 通过数据、训练、推理三阶段联合优化，SWE-TRACE成功平衡了效果与效率，为长时域复杂软件工程任务的自主智能体提供了可行的工程化方案。

Abstract: Resolving real-world software engineering (SWE) issues with autonomous agents requires complex, long-horizon reasoning. Current pipelines are bottlenecked by unoptimized demonstration data, sparse execution rewards, and computationally prohibitive inference scaling, which collectively exacerbate token bloat, reward hacking, and policy degradation. We present SWE-TRACE (Trajectory Reduction and Agentic Criteria Evaluation), a unified framework optimizing the SWE agent lifecycle across data curation, reinforcement learning (RL), and test-time inference. First, we introduce an LLM multi-task cascading method, utilizing stepwise oracle verification to distill a 60K-instance Supervised Fine-Tuning (SFT) corpus strictly biased toward token-efficient, shortest-path trajectories. Second, to overcome the instability of sparse outcome rewards, we design a MemoryAugmented Agentic RL pipeline featuring a Rubric-Based Process Reward Model (PRM). An auxiliary Rubric-Agent provides dense, fine-grained heuristic feedback on intermediate steps, guiding the model through long-horizon tasks. Finally, we bridge training and inference by repurposing the PRM for heuristic-guided Test-Time Scaling (TTS). By dynamically evaluating and pruning action candidates at each step, SWE-TRACE achieves superior search efficiency without the latency overhead of standard parallel sampling. Extensive experiments on standard SWE benchmarks demonstrate that SWE-TRACE significantly advances the state-of-the-art, maximizing resolution rates while drastically reducing both token consumption and inference latency.

</details>


### [78] [Vibe-Coding: Feedback-Based Automated Verification with no Human Code Inspection, a Feasibility Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14867)
*Michal Töpfer,František Plášil,Tomáš Bureš,Petr Hnětynka*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文研究 vibe coding（氛围编程）在运行时自适应系统中的可靠性，提出将自适应循环与 vibe coding 反馈循环结合，通过架构约束和 FCL（一种新的一阶时序逻辑）形式化功能约束来验证 LLM 生成的适配管理器。Dragon Hunt 案例研究表明，细粒度约束违规能提供可操作的反馈，通常在几次迭代内生成有效的适配管理器，而简单的粗粒度指标反馈则容易停滞。反馈精度是决定 vibe coding 可靠性的主导因素。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统 vibe coding 依赖人工检查代码可靠性，但在运行时自适应系统（尤其是用户无编程技能的领域）中，这一假设面临挑战。LLM 生成的适配管理器在运行时可能失败，且缺乏有效的失败检测和修复机制，亟需自动化验证方法来确保系统正确性。

Method: 提出将自适应循环与 vibe coding 反馈循环相结合的双循环架构：（1）适配循环执行 LLM 生成的代码；（2）反馈循环通过两类约束验证代码正确性——通用架构约束和基于 FCL 的形式化功能约束。FCL 是一种新的一阶时序逻辑，支持在潜在有限轨迹上表达时序属性。通过 Dragon Hunt CAS 案例研究评估反馈精度的影响，对比细粒度约束违规反馈与粗粒度指标反馈的效果。

Result: 细粒度约束违规反馈通常在几次迭代内生成有效的适配管理器，而粗粒度指标反馈往往导致修复停滞。这表明反馈精度是 vibe coding 在无编程技能领域专家设计系统中的主导因素，可能无需人工代码检查即可实现可靠自动化。

Conclusion: 反馈精度而非反馈量是 vibe coding 可靠性的关键决定因素。通过形式化约束（FCL）实现的可执行错误报告能够提供足够精确的反馈，使 LLM 自主修复生成代码中的错误，从而在运行时自适应系统中实现可靠的 vibe coding，验证了双循环架构在 CAS 中的可行性。

Abstract: Vibe coding inherently assumes iterative refinement of LLM-generated code through feedback loops. While effective for conventional software tasks, its reliability in runtime-adaptive systems is unclear -- especially when generated code is not manually inspected. This paper studies feedback-based automated verification of LLM-generated adaptation managers in Collective Adaptive Systems (CAS). We focus on the key challenges of verification in the loop: how to detect failures of generated code at runtime and how to report them precisely enough for an LLM to fix them.
  We combine the adaptation loop with a vibe-coding feedback loop where correctness is checked against (i) generic architectural constraints and (ii) functional constraints formalized in Functional Constraints Logic (FCL), a novel first-order temporal logic over potentially finite traces. Conducting the Dragon Hunt CAS case study, we show that fine-grained constraint violations provide actionable feedback that typically yields a valid adaptation manager within a few iterations, while simple coarse metric-based feedback often stalls. Our findings suggest that feedback precision is the dominant factor for reliable vibe coding in systems designed by domain experts with no programming skills, thereby obviating the need for human code inspection.

</details>


### [79] [Towards Understanding Android APIs: Official Lists, Vendor Customizations, and Real-World Usage](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14943)
*Sinan Wang,Qi Zhang,Jiacheng Li,Lili Wei,Yida Tao,Yepang Liu*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 这是首个对四个广泛使用的官方Android API列表（AAL）进行深度实证比较的研究。研究揭示了不同AAL之间的重大差异及其对17,759个真实应用研究的影响，指出先前研究忽视了厂商定制API的广泛使用，并提供了选择和使用AAL的可操作建议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Android应用构建于抽象核心系统功能的API之上，这些API记录在官方文档中（称为AAL）。先前研究常将不同AAL视为可互换的真相，但近期研究表明不同AAL可能导致截然不同的研究结果，引发对Android API分析有效性和可复现性的担忧。

Method: 通过四种方式进行研究：（1）系统表征四个官方AAL的内容及其跨Android版本的演变；（2）细粒度比较各AAL中的API以揭示其API包含策略和差异；（3）在9个真实Android设备（包括原生Android和厂商定制系统）上测试API可用性；（4）对17,759个真实Android应用（包括开源应用、商业应用和恶意软件）进行API使用分析。

Result: 发现官方AAL既不稳定也不相互一致，它们之间的差异会实质性影响研究结论。供应商定制API被正常应用积极使用，却基本上被现有研究忽视。

Conclusion: 研究结果表明官方AAL既不稳定也不一致，差异会显著影响研究结论。供应商定制API被正常应用广泛使用但被现有研究忽视。研究提供了可操作的建议，帮助研究人员更可靠地选择和解释AAL。

Abstract: Android apps are built on APIs that abstract core Android system functionalities. These APIs are officially documented in multiple files distributed with the Android source code or SDK, which we collectively refer to as Android API Lists (AALs). Prior Android research has relied on specific AALs, often treating them as interchangeable ground truth. However, recent studies suggest that different AALs can lead to substantially different research outcomes, raising concerns about the validity and reproducibility of Android API-based analyses. To address this issue, we present the first in-depth empirical study of four official AALs that are widely used in prior work. We systematically characterize their contents and analyze their evolution across Android releases. We then perform a fine-grained comparison of the APIs recorded in each AAL to uncover their underlying API inclusion policies and inconsistencies. To assess the practical impact of these differences, we further examine API availability on nine Android devices, including both stock Android and vendor-customized systems. Finally, we analyze API usage in 17,759 real-world Android apps (including open-source apps, commercial apps, and malware) to quantify how the choice of AAL affects empirical Android research. Our results reveal that official AALs are neither stable nor mutually consistent, and that discrepancies among them can substantially influence research conclusions. We also observe that vendor-customized APIs are actively used by normal apps, yet remain largely overlooked by existing studies. Based on these findings, we discuss their implications for Android API-based research and provide actionable suggestions to help researchers select and interpret AALs more reliably.

</details>


### [80] [Applying SHAPR in AI-Assisted Research Software Development: Lessons Learnt from Building a Share Trading System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15020)
*Ka Ching Chan*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: SHAPR框架旨在解决AI辅助研究软件开发中常见的连续性、可追溯性和方法论清晰度不足的问题。本文通过开发模块化股票交易系统的案例研究，展示了如何系统化地应用SHAPR框架进行AI辅助开发，并提炼出五个关键经验教训和实用工具配置建议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式AI正在改变研究软件的开发方式，但快速的AI辅助开发可能会削弱项目的连续性、可追溯性和方法论清晰度。研究需要一个系统化的框架来指导AI辅助的研究软件开发实践。

Method: 采用案例研究方法，将SHAPR框架应用于模块化股票交易系统的开发项目。从项目启动就采用SHAPR知情的工作配置，通过迭代开发周期生成结构化的证据库，包括反思笔记、开发周期审查笔记、权威源文档、契约、快速捕获、工作流笔记和不断演进的代码制品。

Result: 识别出五个经验教训：(1)契约稳定AI辅助编码；(2)维护权威源文档层提高一致性；(3)周期边界快照加强连续性；(4)代码和文档通过快速捕获和迭代改进共同演化；(5)环境设置本身也有助于知识生成。展示了实用的SHAPR操作配置：ChatGPT Projects和周期特定聊天用于交互和协作，PyCharm支持制品实现，Obsidian支持外部工作记忆和结构化文档。

Conclusion: SHAPR框架为研究人员进行AI辅助研究软件开发提供了实用的指导原则和良好实践。案例研究证明了持续文档更新、快速捕获和AI辅助改进相结合可以维持有序且可用的项目知识，同时保持与SHAPR工具无关原则的一致性。

Abstract: Generative AI is changing how research software is developed, but rapid AI-assisted development can weaken continuity, traceability, and methodological clarity. SHAPR (Solo, Human-centred, AI-assisted PRactice) was proposed as a framework for structuring AI-assisted research software development. This paper presents a documented case of applying SHAPR to the development of a modular share trading system. From the outset, the project adopted a SHAPR-informed working configuration that shaped how interaction, implementation, and documentation were organised. Across iterative development cycles, the project generated a structured evidence base including reflection notes, development cycle review notes, source-of-truth documents, contracts, quick captures, workflow notes, and evolving code artefacts. The case showed that continuous documentation updates, supported by quick capture and AI-assisted refinement, helped maintain organised and usable project knowledge throughout development. Five recurring lessons were identified: contracts stabilised AI-assisted coding, a maintained source-of-truth layer improved coherence, cycle-boundary snapshots strengthened continuity, code and documentation co-evolved through quick capture and iterative refinement, and environment setup itself contributed to knowledge generation. The case also illustrates a practical SHAPR operating configuration in which a ChatGPT Project and cycle-specific chats supported interaction, reasoning, summarisation, and coding collaboration, PyCharm supported artefact implementation, and Obsidian supported external working memory, structured documentation, reflection, continuity, and repository-oriented note organisation, while remaining consistent with SHAPR's tool-agnostic principle. The paper contributes practical guidance and good practices for researchers conducting AI-assisted research software development.

</details>


### [81] [HintPilot: LLM-based Compiler Hint Synthesis for Code Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15041)
*Hanyun Jiang,Peisen Yao,Kaiyue Li,Tingting Lin,Chengpeng Wang,Kui Ren*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: HintPilot是一个将大语言模型（LLM）与传统编译器基础设施相结合的系统，通过合成编译器提示（hints）来优化代码。该方法利用编译器文档进行检索增强合成，并采用性能分析引导的迭代优化，在保持程序正确性的同时实现了最高6.88倍的加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 代码优化是软件开发的核心目标，但现代编译器难以有效探索庞大的优化空间。现有LLM优化方法存在引入语义错误和错失细粒度编译器级优化机会的问题。

Method: HintPilot通过以下技术手段实现代码优化：1) 合成编译器提示（hints），即引导编译器行为的注释；2) 基于编译器文档进行检索增强合成；3) 采用性能分析引导的迭代优化策略。

Result: 在PolyBench和HumanEval-CPP基准测试中，HintPilot相比-Ofast实现了最高6.88倍的几何平均加速，同时保持了程序正确性。

Conclusion: HintPilot成功弥合了LLM推理与传统编译器基础设施之间的鸿沟，为自动化代码优化提供了一种语义保持且有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Code optimization remains a core objective in software development, yet modern compilers struggle to navigate the enormous optimization spaces. While recent research has looked into employing large language models (LLMs) to optimize source code directly, these techniques can introduce semantic errors and miss fine-grained compiler-level optimization opportunities. We present HintPilot, which bridges LLM-based reasoning with traditional compiler infrastructures via synthesizing compiler hints, annotations that steer compiler behavior. HintPilot employs retrieval-augmented synthesis over compiler documentation and applies profiling-guided iterative refinement to synthesize semantics-preserving and effective hints. Upon PolyBench and HumanEval-CPP benchmarks, HintPilot achieves up to 6.88x geometric mean speedup over -Ofast while preserving program correctness.

</details>


### [82] [From Procedural Skills to Strategy Genes: Towards Experience-Driven Test-Time Evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15097)
*Junjie Wang,Yiming Ren,Haoyang Zhang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文研究可复用经验（experience）的表示形式，发现紧凑的Gene表示优于文档导向的Skill包，能更有效地支持测试时控制和迭代演化，在CritPt基准上分别提升9.1%→18.57%和17.7%→27.14%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前AI系统在测试时缺乏有效的经验复用机制。核心问题不是"如何提供更多经验"，而是"如何编码经验为紧凑、可控、演化就绪的对象"。

Method: 在45个科学代码解决场景中进行4,590次受控实验，对比Skill包（文档导向）和Gene表示（紧凑结构）在测试时控制和迭代累积两种设置下的表现。

Result: Gene表示在总体平均性能、结构扰动鲁棒性、匹配预算对比中均优于Skill包；附加失败历史时，Gene的可编辑结构比内容本身更重要；失败信息应蒸馏为紧凑警告而非简单追加。

Conclusion: 经验复用的核心瓶颈在于表示形式本身，而非经验数量；紧凑的Gene表示是更优的载体，能同时支持测试时控制和迭代演化。

Abstract: This beta technical report asks how reusable experience should be represented so that it can function as effective test-time control and as a substrate for iterative evolution. We study this question in 4.590 controlled trials across 45 scientific code-solving scenarios. We find that documentation-oriented Skill packages provide unstable control: their useful signal is sparse, and expanding a compact experience object into a fuller documentation package often fails to help and can degrade the overall average. We further show that representation itself is a first-order factor. A compact Gene representation yields the strongest overall average, remains competitive under substantial structural perturbations, and outperforms matched-budget Skill fragments, while reattaching documentation-oriented material usually weakens rather than improves it. Beyond one-shot control, we show that Gene is also a better carrier for iterative experience accumulation: attached failure history is more effective in Gene than in Skill or freeform text, editable structure matters beyond content alone, and failure information is most useful when distilled into compact warnings rather than naively appended. On CritPt, gene-evolved systems improve over their paired base models from 9.1% to 18.57% and from 17.7% to 27.14%. These results suggest that the core problem in experience reuse is not how to supply more experience, but how to encode experience as a compact, control-oriented, evolution-ready object.

</details>


### [83] [AI-Assisted Requirements Engineering: An Empirical Evaluation Relative to Expert Judgment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15222)
*Oz Levy,Ilya Dikman,Natan Levy,Michael Winokur*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文研究了AI工具在需求工程中评估需求质量的能力，通过对照实验对比AI评估与人类专家基于INCOSE标准的评估效果，发现AI适合快速初步评估，但无法替代专家的上下文理解和权衡推理能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着AI在系统工程中的引入增加，AI工具在需求质量评估与验证方面的作用及其与INCOSE标准的对齐尚未被充分理解，需要实证研究来验证AI辅助评估的有效性与局限性。

Method: 采用结构化系统工程方法论，开展对照研究，由资深系统工程师和AI评估工具分别依据INCOSE"良好需求"标准对系统需求进行评估，重点考察一致性、完整性、清晰性和可测试性，并分析评估的决策逻辑。

Result: AI工具在语法和结构质量属性方面可提供一致且快速的初步评估，但在上下文解读、歧义消解和权衡推理方面仍需专家判断。

Conclusion: AI应定位为需求工程生命周期中的决策支持工具而非替代品，本研究为在保持可追溯性、问责制和工程一致性的前提下将AI整合入RE工作流程提供了实证依据。

Abstract: Artificial Intelligence is increasingly introduced into systems engineering activities, particularly within requirements engineering, where quality assessment and validation remain heavily dependent on expert judgment. While recent AI tools demonstrate promising capabilities in analyzing and generating requirements, their role within formal systems engineering processes-and their alignment with established INCOSE criteria-remains insufficiently understood. This paper investigates the extent to which AI-based tools can support systems engineers in evaluating requirement quality, without replacing professional expertise. The research adopts a structured systems engineering methodology to compare AI-assisted requirement evaluation with human expert assessment. A controlled study was conducted in which system requirements were evaluated against established INCOSE ``good requirement'' criteria by both experienced systems engineers and an AI-based assessment tool. The evaluation focused on consistency, completeness, clarity, and testability, examining not only accuracy but also the decision logic underlying each assessment. Results indicate that AI tools can provide consistent and rapid preliminary assessments, particularly for syntactic and structural quality attributes. However, expert judgment remains essential for contextual interpretation, ambiguity resolution, and trade-off reasoning. Rather than positioning AI as a replacement for systems engineers, the findings support its role as a decision-support mechanism within the RE lifecycle. From a systems engineering perspective, this study contributes empirical evidence on how AI can be integrated into RE workflows while preserving traceability, accountability, and engineering consistency.

</details>


### [84] [Enhancing Large Language Models with Retrieval Augmented Generation for Software Testing and Inspection Automation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15270)
*Zoe Fingleton,Nazanin Siavash,Armin Moin*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出利用大语言模型（LLM）自动化软件开发生命周期中的验证与确认（V&V）活动，包括自动生成测试用例和源代码审查，并通过检索增强生成（RAG）管道解决LLM幻觉问题，实验结果表明该方法有效提升了V&V活动的效能和效率，降低了项目成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软件测试和代码审查是软件开发生命周期中成本高昂且耗时的关键V&V活动。传统方法依赖人工执行，效率有限。同时，LLM在自动化这些任务时存在"幻觉"问题——即自信地生成错误输出，这严重制约了其在安全关键软件中的应用。

Method: 研究采用检索增强生成（RAG）管道，为LLM整合外部补充知识源并提供额外上下文。具体应用于两个场景：基于LLM的自动测试用例生成，以及基于LLM的源代码审查。

Result: 实验结果表明，通过RAG管道引入外部上下文对测试用例生成和代码审查均产生积极影响。该方法不仅节省了人工测试/审查人员的时间，降低了项目总成本，还提升了V&V活动的有效性和效率。

Conclusion: RAG增强的LLM方法为软件V&V活动的自动化提供了一条可行路径，能够有效应对LLM幻觉挑战，并在实际项目中展现出成本节约和效能提升的双重价值。

Abstract: In this paper, we focus on automating two of the widely used Verification and Validation (V&V) activities in the Software Development Lifecycle (SDLC): Software testing and software inspection (also known as review). Concerning the former, we concentrate on automated test case generation using Large Language Models (LLMs). For the latter, we enable inspection of the source code by LLMs. To address the known LLM hallucination problem, in which LLMs confidently produce incorrect outputs, we implement a Retrieval Augmented Generation (RAG) pipeline to integrate supplementary knowledge sources and provide additional context to the LLM. Our experimental results indicate that incorporating external context via the RAG pipeline has a generally positive impact on both test case generation and code inspection. This novel approach reduces the total project cost by saving human testers'/inspectors' time. It also improves the effectiveness and efficiency of these V&V activities, as evidenced by our experimental study.

</details>


<div id='cs.DB'></div>

# cs.DB [[Back]](#toc)

### [85] [Parallel R-tree-based Spatial Query Processing on a Commercial Processing-in-Memory System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14445)
*Tasmia Jannat,Michael Gowanlock,Satish Puri*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 首个在商业PIM硬件上实现R树范围查询的研究，通过广播式方法在UPMEM DRAM处理单元上执行并行空间查询，在湖泊数据集上实现3.66倍内核加速和2.70倍端到端加速，同时能耗降低3.4倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学领域数据量增长导致空间查询处理面临内存层次间高数据传输成本和有限内存带宽的瓶颈，需减少数据移动带来的能耗。

Method: 提出广播式方法：1) 在CPU上自底向上构建R树；2) 将顶层广播至UPMEM DPUs进行全局过滤；3) 分发低层至DPUs进行并行批量查询。在UPMEM PIM系统（最多2540个DPUs）上评估。

Result: 广播式执行始终优于子树划分。Lakes数据集上，512至2540 DPUs强扩展将内核时间从64.9s降至17.6s。相比同系统CPU R树搜索，内核加速3.66倍，端到端加速2.70倍。内核能耗降低约3.4倍（59.6 kJ vs 167.0 kJ）。

Conclusion: 广播式PIM执行实现了可扩展且节能的层次化空间范围查询，验证了R树范围查询在商业PIM硬件上的可行性和性能优势。

Abstract: The growing volume of data in scientific domains has made spatial query processing increasingly challenging due to high data transfer costs across the memory hierarchy and limited memory bandwidth. To address these bottlenecks and reduce the energy consumed on data movement, this work explores Processing-in-Memory (PIM) systems by executing range queries directly inside memory chips. Unlike prior PIM studies centered on linear scans or hash-based queries, this work is the first to map R-tree range queries onto commercial PIM hardware. The proposed broadcast-based method constructs the R-tree bottom-up on the CPU, broadcasts top levels to UPMEM DPUs (DRAM Processing Units) for global filtering, and distributes lower levels for parallel batched queries in a CPU-DPU system. We evaluate our approach on two real spatial datasets, Sports (999K rectangles) and Lakes (8.4M rectangles), and assess scalability using a synthetic dataset with up to 16M rectangles and 3.9M queries on a commercial UPMEM PIM system with up to 2,540 DPUs. Across all datasets, broadcast-based execution consistently outperforms subtree partitioning by preventing communication from dominating execution. On the Lakes dataset, strong scaling from 512 to 2,540 DPUs reduces kernel time from 64.9 s to 17.6 s, yielding up to 3.66x kernel and 2.70x end-to-end speedup relative to the CPU R-tree search on the same system. The PIM kernel also consumes approximately 3.4x less energy than the corresponding CPU search (e.g., 59.6 kJ vs. 167.0 kJ on Lakes), demonstrating scalable and energy-efficient hierarchical spatial range queries.

</details>


### [86] [RELOAD: A Robust and Efficient Learned Query Optimizer for Database Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14725)
*Seokwon Lee,Jaeyoung Sim,Sihyun Kim,Yuhsing Li,Yiwen Zhu,Kwanghyun Park*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: RELOAD是一种针对数据库系统的鲁棒高效学习型查询优化器，通过强化学习技术解决现有RL-based查询优化器存在的性能不稳定、严重性能回退及训练时间长等问题，在标准基准测试中展现出2.4倍鲁棒性和3.1倍效率提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于强化学习的查询优化器存在两大核心缺陷：(1) 单查询级别性能不稳定，存在严重的性能回退；(2) 需要长时间训练才能达到专家级成本优化器的方案质量。这些问题阻碍了学习型查询优化器在生产数据库系统中的实际部署和应用。

Method: RELOAD采用强化学习框架，专注于两个核心目标：(i) 鲁棒性 - 通过最小化查询级性能回退，确保跨执行的一致优化行为；(ii) 高效性 - 加速收敛至专家级方案质量。

Result: 在Join Order Benchmark、TPC-DS和Star Schema Benchmark等标准基准测试中，RELOAD相比现有最先进的RL查询优化技术，实现了最高2.4倍的鲁棒性提升和3.1倍的效率提升。

Conclusion: RELOAD有效地解决了学习型查询优化器在鲁棒性和效率方面的关键挑战，为生产级数据库系统中部署机器学习驱动的查询优化技术提供了可行的解决方案。

Abstract: Recent advances in query optimization have shifted from traditional rule-based and cost-based techniques towards machine learning-driven approaches. Among these, reinforcement learning (RL) has attracted significant attention due to its ability to optimize long-term performance by learning policies over query planning. However, existing RL-based query optimizers often exhibit unstable performance at the level of individual queries, including severe performance regressions, and require prolonged training to reach the plan quality of expert, cost-based optimizers. These shortcomings make learned query optimizers difficult to deploy in practice and remain a major barrier to their adoption in production database systems. To address these challenges, we present RELOAD, a robust and efficient learned query optimizer for database systems. RELOAD focuses on (i) robustness, by minimizing query-level performance regressions and ensuring consistent optimization behavior across executions, and (ii) efficiency, by accelerating convergence to expert-level plan quality. Through extensive experiments on standard benchmarks, including Join Order Benchmark, TPC-DS, and Star Schema Benchmark, RELOAD demonstrates up to 2.4x higher robustness and 3.1x greater efficiency compared to state-of-the-art RL-based query optimization techniques.

</details>


### [87] [Data Engineering Patterns for Cross-System Reconciliation in Regulated Enterprises: Architecture, Anomaly Detection, and Governance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15108)
*Zhijun Qiu*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 本文提出了GERA框架，一种用于解决受监管企业系统碎片化问题的四层数据架构，整合了对账、异常检测、语义标准化和安全控制，已在多个受监管行业经过实践验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 美国受监管企业（银行、电信、大型科技公司）存在系统碎片化问题——ERP平台、计费引擎、供应链工具和财务报告基础设施之间缺乏互操作性，导致交易未对账、资产清单与系统记录不一致、审计依赖手动工作。PCAOB 2024年检查显示受检公司Part I.A缺陷率高达39%。

Method: 提出了GERA框架（Governed Enterprise Reconciliation Architecture），这是一个供应商中立的四层数据架构，包含：确定性跨系统对账、统计异常检测（基线Z分数及稳健替代方案）、治理语义标准化，以及NIST CSF 2.0对齐的安全控制。架构涵盖四层：摄取层、暂存层、核心模型层和语义服务层。

Result: 通过美国宽带运营场景（计费对账、库存老化、治理紧密耦合）演示了该架构模式，这些模式已在三个受监管企业环境中经过领域测试。

Conclusion: GERA框架是一个实践者参考文档，记录了跨银行、宽带提供商和财富500强科技公司金融组织的领域测试模式，提供了一种供应商中立的方法论来解决受监管企业的系统碎片化问题，而不披露专有系统或内部实现。

Abstract: Regulated enterprises in the United States -- banks, telecommunications providers, large technology companies -- operate across heterogeneous systems that were rarely designed to interoperate. ERP platforms, billing engines, supply chain tools, and financial reporting infrastructure coexist within the same organization, but they do not talk to each other well. The resulting fragmentation produces familiar problems: transactions recorded in one system but unreconciled in another, asset inventories drifting from their systems of record, and audit-readiness that depends on manual effort. The PCAOB's 2024 inspection cycle put a number on the consequences: a 39% aggregate Part I.A deficiency rate across all inspected firms. This paper introduces the GERA Framework (Governed Enterprise Reconciliation Architecture) -- a vendor-neutral, four-layer data architecture that integrates deterministic cross-system reconciliation, statistical anomaly detection (baseline Z-Score with robust alternatives), governed semantic standardization, and NIST CSF 2.0-aligned security controls into a single methodology. The architecture spans four layers (ingestion, staging, core models, and semantic serving), following the multi-layer pattern now common in modern data platforms. The patterns are demonstrated through U.S. broadband operations -- where billing reconciliation, inventory aging, and governance are tightly coupled -- and draw on the author's implementation experience across three regulated enterprise environments: a regional bank, a national broadband provider, and a Fortune 500 technology company's central finance organization. This is a practitioner reference -- an architectural framework paper documenting field-tested patterns -- not a controlled experiment or benchmark study. No proprietary systems, datasets, or internal implementations are disclosed.

</details>


### [88] [DPC: Training-Free Text-to-SQL Candidate Selection via Dual-Paradigm Consistency](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15163)
*Boyan Li,Ou Ocean Kun Hei,Yue Yu,Yuyu Luo*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: DPC提出了一个多智能体框架，通过在可见数据上构建最小区分数据库（MDD）来将SQL选择从概率猜测任务转变为确定性验证任务，并通过跨范式验证（SQL vs Python）来打破自我修正偏差，实现更准确的SQL候选筛选。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM生成SQL时存在"生成-选择差距"（Generation-Selection Gap），即Pass@K的高潜力准确率无法转化为Pass@1的执行准确率。现有方法如监督验证器成本高且泛化差，而训练-free方法存在系统性偏差（共识幻觉）和符号盲区（无法模拟执行状态）。

Method: DPC采用双范式一致性框架，包含三个协作智能体：SLICER和TESTER负责在可见数据上构建最小区分数据库（MDD）作为对抗性微环境暴露候选SQL间的逻辑差异；SOLVER通过将SQL执行与并行Python/Pandas解决方案交叉引用来验证候选SQL，从而通过声明式（SQL）与命令式（Python）范式的执行一致性来辨别正确逻辑。

Result: 在BIRD和Spider数据集上针对多个LLM的实验表明，DPC始终优于现有选择基线方法，相比Self-Consistency等强竞争对手实现了最高2.2%的绝对准确率提升。

Conclusion: 通过将SQL选择重新定义为可见数据上的确定性验证任务，并利用跨范式执行一致性，DPC有效解决了LLM在SQL选择中的自我修正偏差问题，为训练-free的LLM自我评估提供了新范式。

Abstract: While Large Language Models (LLMs) demonstrate impressive proficiency in generating SQL queries, they fundamentally lack the capability to self-evaluate correctness without an execution oracle. This limitation creates a stark Generation-Selection Gap, where high potential accuracy (Pass@K) fails to translate into execution accuracy (Pass@1). Although supervised verifiers offer mitigation, they incur prohibitive annotation costs and suffer from domain fragility. Consequently, recent research has pivoted to the training-free setting. However, existing methods--such as Self-Consistency or LLM-as-a-Judge--remain hampered by systematic bias (consensus on hallucinations) and symbolic blindness (inability to simulate execution states). We introduce DPC (Dual-Paradigm Consistency), a multi-agent framework that reformulates SQL selection from a probabilistic guessing task on hidden data into a deterministic verification task on visible data. Specifically, DPC employs a SLICER and a TESTER agent to collaboratively construct a Minimal Distinguishing Database (MDD)--an adversarial, fully observable micro-environment engineered to expose logical discrepancies between candidates. To break the self-correction bias, a SOLVER agent then verifies the SQL candidates by cross-referencing their execution against a parallel Python/Pandas solution. By validating execution consistency between declarative (SQL) and imperative (Python) paradigms, DPC robustly discriminates correct logic from systematic hallucinations. Experiments on BIRD and Spider across multiple LLMs demonstrate that our method consistently outperforms existing selection baselines, achieving absolute accuracy improvements of up to 2.2% over strong competitors like Self-Consistency.

</details>


<div id='cs.DC'></div>

### [89] [Incidence Constraints in Hypergraph Partitioning on GPU](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14411)
*Marco Ronzani,Cristina Silvano*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: Hypergraph partitioning is a pervasive NP-hard problem, and accelerating its computation on GPU can both slice time-to-solution and raise quality of results. In this work, we implement a multi-level hypergraph partitioning algorithm on GPU targeting a specific set of problem constraints: bounded per-partition size and distinct inbound hyperedges. Manipulating hypergraphs requires long orders of nested iterations, and enforcing these constraints introduces further set operations amidst them. Hence, we design algorithms around our problem's specifics, materializing the hypergraph's incidence structure in memory and exploiting set sparsity. Our results show competitive speedups as high as 940x and 2-26% better results in connectivity over a sequential multi-level partitioner.

</details>


### [90] [CoCoDiff: Optimizing Collective Communications for Distributed Diffusion Transformer Inference Under Ulysses Sequence Parallelism](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14561)
*Bin Ma,Xingjian Ding,Tekin Bicer,Pengfei Su,Dong Li*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: CoCoDiff 是一个针对 Diffusion Transformers (DiTs) 的分布式推理引擎，通过三种机制优化多 GPU 推理：Tile-Aware Parallel All-to-all (TAPA)、V-First 调度和 V-Major 选择性通信，在 Aurora 超算上实现最高 8.4x 加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DiT 模型规模增大和分辨率提升使得多 GPU 分布式推理成为必需，但 Ulysses 序列并行引入的频繁 all-to-all 集合通信主导了延迟，且由于紧耦合的数据依赖、大消息量和非对称互连带宽，很难与计算重叠。

Method: CoCoDiff 利用两个关键观察：(1) V 投影只需线性操作，而 Q/K 需额外归一化和 RoPE，为 V 通信与 Q/K 计算重叠创造机会；(2) 相邻去噪步骤产生相似张量，呈现时间冗余。基于此提出三种机制：TAPA 将集合通信分解为拓扑对齐的阶段；V-First 调度将 V 通信隐藏在 Q/K 计算之后；V-Major 选择性通信仅在慢速互连上传输活跃投影。

Result: 在 Aurora 超算上，使用四个 DiT 模型、1-8 节点（最多 96 个 Intel GPU tile）进行测试，CoCoDiff 实现平均 3.6x 加速，峰值达 8.4x。

Conclusion: 通过挖掘 V/Q/K 计算特性差异和时间冗余性，CoCoDiff 能有效隐藏通信开销，显著提升分布式 DiT 推理效率。

Abstract: Diffusion Transformers (DiTs) are increasingly adopted in scientific computing, yet growing model sizes and resolutions make distributed multi-GPU inference essential. Ulysses sequence parallelism scales DiT inference but introduces frequent all-to-all collectives that dominate latency. Overlapping these with computation is difficult due to tight data dependencies, large message volumes, and asymmetric interconnect bandwidths.
  We introduce CoCoDiff, a distributed DiT inference engine exploiting two observations: (1) V requires only linear projection while Q/K need additional normalization and RoPE, creating opportunities to overlap V's communication with Q/K computation; (2) adjacent denoising steps produce similar tensors, yielding temporal redundancy. CoCoDiff introduces three mechanisms: Tile-Aware Parallel All-to-all (TAPA) decomposes collectives into topology-aligned phases; V-First scheduling hides V's communication behind Q/K computation; and V-Major selective communication transmits only active projections on slow interconnects. On the Aurora supercomputer with four DiT models across 1-8 nodes (up to 96 Intel GPU tiles), CoCoDiff achieves an average speedup of 3.6x, peaking at 8.4x.

</details>


### [91] [AgileLog: A Forkable Shared Log for Agents on Data Streams](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14590)
*Shreesha G. Bhat,Tony Hong,Michael Noguera,Ramnatthan Alagappan,Aishwarya Ganesan*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出 AgileLog 共享日志抽象，通过提供分叉原语支持 AI 智能体在流数据上运行，解决了传统流系统无法避免智能体任务性能干扰和安全处理智能体写入的问题。Bolt 是 AgileLog 的实现，采用新技术使分叉操作轻量化，并提供逻辑和性能隔离。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代数据流系统面临新挑战：AI 智能体作为新型实体通过 LLM 推理在流数据上执行自然语言指定的高级任务，但现有流系统缺乏两个关键机制：避免智能体任务造成的性能干扰，以及安全处理智能体写入操作。这限制了 AI 智能体与流数据的深度交互能力。

Method: 提出名为 AgileLog 的新型共享日志抽象，提供针对智能体场景的创新分叉原语。实现方案 Bolt 采用以下技术：(1) 新型技术使分叉操作轻量化；(2) 提供逻辑隔离；(3) 提供性能隔离。

Result: 构建了可分叉的共享日志系统，作为 AI 智能体在流数据上操作的理想底层基础设施。分叉机制使每个智能体可获得独立的日志视图，避免相互干扰，同时保持底层流数据的共享特性。

Conclusion: 可分叉的共享日志能够有效支持流数据上的智能体操作，通过分叉原语实现逻辑隔离和性能隔离，为 AI 智能体与流数据的交互提供了统一、安全、高效的基础抽象。

Abstract: In modern data-streaming systems, alongside traditional programs, a new type of entity has emerged that can interact with streaming data: AI agents. Unlike traditional programs, AI agents use LLM reasoning to accomplish high-level tasks specified in natural language over streaming data. Unfortunately, current streaming systems cannot fully support agents: they lack the fundamental mechanisms to avoid the performance interference caused by agentic tasks and to safely handle agentic writes. We argue that the shared log, the core abstraction underlying streaming data, must support creating forks of itself, and that such a forkable shared log serves as a great substrate for agents acting on streaming data. We propose AgileLog, a new shared log abstraction that provides novel forking primitives for agentic use cases. We design Bolt, an implementation of the AgileLog abstraction, that uses novel techniques to make forks cheap, and provide logical and performance isolation.

</details>


### [92] [Serving Chain-structured Jobs with Large Memory Footprints with Application to Large Foundation Model Serving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14993)
*Tingyang Sun,Ting He,I-Hong Hou*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文研究大型基础模型服务系统中的资源管理问题，提出"服务器链组合"问题（通过块放置和缓存分配优化链结构作业），证明其NP难性，并设计可扩展算法应用于分布式LLM服务系统，显著降低响应时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型基础模型即使在训练完成后，由于其巨大的GPU内存占用，在规模化服务时仍面临严峻挑战。现有的分布式计算系统理论无法充分解释基础模型服务系统的独特特征，缺乏对底层系统管理问题的基本理解。

Method: 1) 将问题形式化为"服务器链组合"问题，通过块放置和缓存分配优化链结构作业的服务；2) 证明最优解的NP难性；3) 基于先进负载均衡策略设计可扩展算法；4) 在分布式LLM服务系统中验证方案效果。

Result: 所提算法在响应时间上相比现有最优方案有显著降低。

Conclusion: 服务器链组合问题建模了流水线并行服务大型基础模型的基本资源管理问题，所设计的可扩展算法在实际系统中表现优异，为基础模型服务系统的优化提供了新的理论基础和实践方案。

Abstract: As a current trend in Artificial Intelligence (AI), large foundation models are increasingly employed as the core of AI services. However, even after training, serving such models at scale remains a challenging task due to their heavy resource footprints, particularly in terms of GPU memory. While recent works revealed unique characteristics of systems serving foundation models that distinguish them from traditional distributed computing systems, there is still a lack of fundamental understanding of the underlying system management problems. This work aims at addressing this gap by extracting a novel problem of "server chain composition" via block placement and cache allocation for serving chainstructured jobs with large memory footprints, which models a fundamental problem in serving large foundation models through pipeline parallelism. After showing the NP-hardness of the optimal solution, the focus is turned to developing scalable algorithms with guaranteed performance under state-of-the-art load balancing. Application of the proposed solution to a distributed large language model (LLM) serving system shows significant reduction of response times compared to state-of-the-art solutions.

</details>


### [93] [Efficient calculation of available space for multi-NUMA virtual machines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15033)
*Andrei Gudkov,Elizaveta Ponomareva,Alexis Pospelov*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 云服务提供商使用多-NUMA服务器和虚拟机，但多-NUMA VM的调度需要将虚拟NUMA拓扑映射到物理NUMA拓扑，这是一个组合优化问题。本文推导了封闭形式表达式，用于计算在4/8-NUMA物理拓扑上可分配的最大2/4-NUMA对称VM数量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 云计算中多-NUMA服务器和虚拟机的使用增加了调度算法的复杂性，调度器需要在选择VM主机的同时，还要将虚拟NUMA拓扑映射到物理NUMA拓扑上，以优化VM性能并最小化干扰，这导致最大化多-NUMA VM分配成为一个组合优化问题。

Method: 推导了封闭形式表达式来计算给定flavor的最大可分配VM数量，考虑了2-NUMA和4-NUMA对称VM映射到4-NUMA和8-NUMA物理拓扑的非平凡场景。

Result: 获得可计算最大VM分配数量的封闭形式表达式。

Conclusion: 研究结果具有广泛适用性，可应用于实时仪表板（显示每种VM flavor的可用集群容量）和大规模云资源重组的优化工具。

Abstract: Increasing demand for computational power has led cloud providers to employ multi-NUMA servers and offer multi-NUMA virtual machines to their customers. However, multi-NUMA VMs introduce additional complexity to scheduling algorithms. Beyond merely selecting a host for a VM, the scheduler has to map virtual NUMA topology onto the physical NUMA topology of the server to ensure optimal VM performance and minimize interference with co-located VMs. Under these constraints, maximizing the number of allocated multi-NUMA VMs on a host becomes a combinatorial optimization problem. In this paper, we derive closed-form expressions to compute the maximum number of VMs for a given flavor that can be additionally allocated onto a physical server. We consider nontrivial scenarios of mapping 2- and 4-NUMA symmetric VMs to 4- and 8-NUMA physical topologies. Our results have broad applicability, ranging from real-time dashboards (displaying available cluster capacity per VM flavor) to optimization tools for large-scale cloud resource reorganization.

</details>


### [94] [Scepsy: Serving Agentic Workflows Using Aggregate LLM Pipelines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15186)
*Marcel Wagenländer,Otto White,Britannio Jarrett,Pedro Silvestre,Yanda Tao,Guo Li,Huanzhou Zhu,Llúis Vilanova,Peter Pietzuch*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: Scepsy is an agentic serving system that efficiently schedules arbitrary multi-LLM workflows onto GPU clusters by leveraging the insight that aggregate LLM execution time shares are comparatively stable across executions. It uses profiling data to construct an Aggregate LLM Pipeline predictor and explores GPU allocation search space with hierarchical heuristics for cluster placement, achieving up to 2.4x throughput improvement and 27x latency reduction.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Serving agentic workflows (orchestrating multiple LLMs and tools) at target throughput with low latency is challenging due to arbitrary agentic framework definitions, unpredictable execution times with data-dependent branching, fan-out, and recurrence patterns, and resulting GPU oversubscription when LLMs outnumber available GPUs.

Method: Scepsy profiles LLMs under different parallelism degrees to construct an Aggregate LLM Pipeline as a lightweight latency/throughput predictor. It explores a search space over fractional GPU shares, tensor parallelism degrees, and replica counts to find optimal GPU allocations, then uses a hierarchical heuristic for cluster placement that minimizes fragmentation while respecting network topology constraints.

Result: Evaluation on realistic agentic workflows shows Scepsy achieves up to 2.4x higher throughput and 27x lower latency compared to systems optimizing LLMs independently or relying on user-specified allocations.

Conclusion: Scepsy successfully enables efficient scheduling of arbitrary multi-LLM agentic workflows onto GPU clusters, achieving significant performance improvements through its novel allocation strategy.

Abstract: Agentic workflows carry out complex tasks by orchestrating multiple large language models (LLMs) and tools. Serving such workflows at a target throughput with low latency is challenging because they can be defined using arbitrary agentic frameworks and exhibit unpredictable execution times: execution may branch, fan-out, or recur in data-dependent ways. Since LLMs in workflows often outnumber available GPUs, their execution also leads to GPU oversubscription.
  We describe Scepsy, a new agentic serving system that efficiently schedules arbitrary multi-LLM agentic workflows onto a GPU cluster. Scepsy exploits the insight that, while agentic workflows have unpredictable end-to-end latencies, the shares of each LLM's total execution times are comparatively stable across executions. Scepsy decides on GPU allocations based on these aggregate shares: first, it profiles the LLMs under different parallelism degrees. It then uses these statistics to construct an Aggregate LLM Pipeline, which is a lightweight latency/throughput predictor for allocations. To find a GPU allocation that minimizes latency while achieving a target throughput, Scepsy uses the Aggregate LLM Pipeline to explore a search space over fractional GPU shares, tensor parallelism degrees, and replica counts. It uses a hierarchical heuristic to place the best allocation onto the GPU cluster, minimizing fragmentation, while respecting network topology constraints. Our evaluation on realistic agentic workflows shows that Scepsy achieves up to 2.4x higher throughput and 27x lower latency compared to systems that optimize LLMs independently or rely on user-specified allocations.

</details>


### [95] [Wave-Based Dispatch for Circuit Cutting in Hybrid HPC--Quantum Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15279)
*Ricard S. García-Raigada,Josep Jorba,Sergio Iserte*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: DQR是一个运行时框架，通过将量子电路片段作为一等可调度单元，将电路切割逻辑与执行编排解耦，从而使HPC中心能够将NISQ工作负载集成到现有生产基础设施中，同时保持对改进的切割算法和异构后端技术的适应性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Existing circuit cutting frameworks tightly couple cutting logic to execution orchestration, which prevents HPC centers from applying mature resource management policies to NISQ workloads. There is a need to decouple circuit decomposition from execution to enable proper scheduling and resource management.

Method: The framework introduces three key mechanisms: (1) a backend-agnostic fragment descriptor that exposes structural properties without requiring execution layers to parse quantum code, (2) a wave-based coordinator achieving pipeline concurrency via non-blocking polling, and (3) a production-ready implementation on CESGA Qmio supercomputer integrating both local QPUs (Qmio) and remote cloud backends (IBM Torino). Per-fragment failover recovery enables transparent rerouting from local QPU to classical simulators upon hardware-level incompatibilities without pipeline restart.

Result: Experiments on a 32-qubit Hardware-Efficient Ansatz (HEA) circuit demonstrate makespan improvements over a monolithic CPU baseline, transparent per-fragment failover recovery (rerouting from local QPU to classical simulators upon hardware incompatibilities without pipeline restart), and coordination overhead accounting for only 5% of total execution time for deeper circuits, highlighting framework scalability.

Conclusion: DQR enables HPC centers to integrate NISQ workloads into existing production infrastructure while preserving flexibility to adopt improved cutting algorithms or heterogeneous backend technologies. The framework demonstrates strong scalability with coordination overhead of only 5% for deeper circuits.

Abstract: Hybrid High-performance Computing (HPC)-quantum workloads based on circuit cutting decompose large quantum circuits into independent fragments, but existing frameworks tightly couple cutting logic to execution orchestration, preventing HPC centers from applying mature resource management policies to Noisy Intermediate-Scale Quantum (NISQ) workloads. We present DQR (Dynamic Queue Router), a runtime framework that bridges this gap by treating circuit fragments as first-class schedulable units. The framework introduces a backend-agnostic fragment descriptor to expose structural properties without requiring execution layers to parse quantum code, a wave-based coordinator that achieves pipeline concurrency via non-blocking polling, and a production-ready implementation on the CESGA Qmio supercomputer integrating both QPUs local on-premises (Qmio) and remote cloud (IBM Torino) backends. Experiments on a 32-qubit Hardware-Efficient Ansatz (HEA) circuit demonstrate not only makespan improvements over a monolithic CPU baseline but also transparent per-fragment failover recovery-specifically rerouting tasks from the local QPU to classical simulators upon encountering hardware-level incompatibilities-without pipeline restart. For deeper circuits, the coordination residual accounts for only 5% of the total execution time, highlighting the framework's scalability. These results show that DQR enables HPC centers to integrate NISQ workloads into existing production infrastructure while preserving the flexibility to adopt improved cutting algorithms or heterogeneous backend technologies.

</details>


### [96] [BitFlipScope: Scalable Fault Localization and Recovery for Bit-Flip Corruptions in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.22174)
*Muhammad Zeeshan Karamat,Sadman Saif,Christiana Chamon Garcia*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: BitFlipScope是一个软件框架，用于在变压器架构中定位由位翻转故障（硬件老化、宇宙辐射、Rowhammer攻击等）引起的LLM损坏区域。框架支持两种场景：有干净参考模型时进行差分分析，无参考模型时使用残差路径扰动和损失敏感度分析进行故障定位，同时支持轻量级性能恢复而无需微调。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在实用和安全关键场景中日益受到位翻转故障的影响，这些故障由硬件老化、宇宙辐射或Rowhammer等攻击引起，会导致模型参数损坏和不可预测的危险行为。由于无法定位故障区域，既无法诊断退化原因，也无法应用有针对性的纠正措施，只能采用昂贵的微调或完全重训练来恢复功能。

Method: BitFlipScope采用两种方法来定位故障区域：1）有干净参考模型时，对输出、隐藏状态和内部激活进行差分分析，检测异常行为；2）无参考模型时，使用残差路径扰动和损失敏感度分析直接从损坏模型推断故障影响区域。此外，框架还支持无需微调的轻量级性能恢复。

Result: BitFlipScope能够有效诊断故障并支持轻量级性能恢复，无需昂贵的微调或完全重训练。

Conclusion: BitFlipScope是实现可信赖、容错LLM部署的重要一步，特别适用于易受硬件故障和对抗性攻击影响的环境。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) deployed in practical and safety-critical settings are increasingly susceptible to bit-flip faults caused by hardware degradation, cosmic radiation, or deliberate fault-injection attacks such as Rowhammer. These faults silently corrupt internal parameters and can lead to unpredictable or dangerous model behavior. Localizing these corruptions is essential: without identifying the affected region, it is impossible to diagnose the source of degradation, apply targeted corrective measures, or restore model functionality without resorting to costly fine-tuning or full retraining. This work introduces BitFlipScope, a scalable, software-based framework for identifying fault-affected regions within transformer architectures under two deployment scenarios. When a clean reference model is available, BitFlipScope performs differential analysis of outputs, hidden states, and internal activations for detecting anomalous behavior indicative of corruption to pinpoint or localize faults. When no reference model exists, it uses residual-path perturbation and loss-sensitivity profiling to infer the fault-impacted region directly from the corrupted model. In both settings, the framework not only enables effective fault diagnosis but also supports lightweight performance recovery without fine-tuning, offering a practical path to restoring corrupted models. Together, these capabilities make BitFlipScope an important step toward trustworthy, fault-resilient LLM deployment in hardware-prone and adversarial environments.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [97] [NuHF Claw: A Risk Constrained Cognitive Agent Framework for Human Centered Procedure Support in Digital Nuclear Control Rooms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14160)
*Xingyu Xiao,Jiejuan Tong,Jun Sun,Zhe Sui,Peng Chen,Jingang Liang,Haitao Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究提出NuHF Claw框架，一种用于数字化核电站运营的持久性认知风险智能体框架，通过风险约束的运行时机制将认知状态推断与概率安全评估紧密耦合，实现从传统离线可靠性分析向主动干预机制的转变，在高保真数字控制室模拟器上验证了其在保持人类决策权威的同时动态约束不安全自主建议的能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 核电站主控室快速数字化引入了复杂的软控制行为和 elevated cognitive risks，现有的人因可靠性分析（HRA）方法无法充分应对。虽然大语言模型和自主智能体为智能决策支持提供了新机遇，但其在安全关键环境中的应用仍受制于推理幻觉风险和人类权威弱化问题。

Method: 提出NuHF Claw持久性认知风险智能体框架，核心创新在于引入风险约束智能体运行时机制，将认知状态推断与概率安全评估紧耦合，实时监管自主系统行为。通过整合认知负荷和态势感知估计与动态人因失误概率预测，将传统离线可靠性分析转化为嵌入操作工作流的主动干预机制。

Result: 在高保真数字控制室模拟器上的实验验证表明，NuHF Claw能够：预见界面诱发的认知退化、动态约束不安全自主建议、提供风险感知导航指导，同时保持人类决策权威。

Conclusion: 该研究实现了从自动化驱动操作向认知感知自主的 fundamental shift，为智能体安全集成到下一代核电站控制环境提供了 principled 路径。

Abstract: The rapid digitization of nuclear power plant main control rooms has fundamentally reshaped operator interaction patterns, introducing complex soft-control behaviors and elevated cognitive risks that are not adequately addressed by existing human reliability analysis approaches. Although recent advances in large language models and autonomous agents offer new opportunities for intelligent decision support, their deployment in safety critical environments remains constrained by risks of hallucinated reasoning and weakened human authority. This study proposes NuHF Claw, a persistent cognitive-risk agent framework that enables risk governed human centered autonomy for digital nuclear operations. The core methodological innovation lies in the introduction of a risk constrained agent runtime, which tightly couples cognitive state inference with probabilistic safety assessment to regulate autonomous system behavior in real time. By integrating cognitively grounded workload and situational awareness estimation with dynamic human error probability prediction, the framework transforms conventional offline reliability analysis into a proactive intervention mechanism embedded directly within operational workflows. Experimental validation on a high-fidelity digital control room simulator demonstrates that NuHF Claw can anticipate interface induced cognitive degradation, dynamically constrain unsafe autonomous recommendations, and provide risk-aware navigational guidance while preserving human decision authority. The results highlight a fundamental shift from automation-driven operation toward cognition-aware autonomy, offering a principled pathway for the safe integration of intelligent agents into next-generation nuclear control environments.

</details>


### [98] [Simulating Human Cognition: Heartbeat-Driven Autonomous Thinking Activity Scheduling for LLM-based AI systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14178)
*Hong Su*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种心跳驱动的自主思考活动调度机制，使LLM智能体能够主动、适应性地进行自我调节。通过周期性心跳机制协调多种认知模块（规划器、评论器、记忆召回器、想象器等），系统能够根据时间模式和历史上下文学习何时激活特定思考活动，而非依赖硬编码规则或被动触发。该方法还包含元学习策略，支持通过历史交互日志持续优化认知策略，并允许动态增减认知模块。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前LLM智能体的控制流程僵化且被动，主要依赖固定管道或失败触发的反思机制，导致智能体行为冲动或仅在错误发生后才进行纠正，严重限制了其适应性和效率。现有方法缺乏主动、持续性的自我调节能力。

Method: 1) 提出周期性"心跳"机制，模拟人类认知的自然节律；2) 设计动态认知模块库（规划、批评、回忆、想象等），可灵活编排；3) 采用学习驱动的方式决定何时激活特定思考活动；4) 引入元学习策略，通过历史交互日志持续优化调度策略；5) 支持功能化模块管理，动态增减模块无需重构系统结构。

Result: 实验结果表明，该方法能有效基于历史数据学习认知活动调度规则，并能够自主集成新的思考模块，验证了所提机制的有效性。

Conclusion: 心跳驱动的自主思考活动调度机制为LLM智能体提供了一种主动、适应性的自我调节范式，通过学习驱动的调度和功能化的模块管理，显著提升了智能体的灵活性和可扩展性。

Abstract: Large Language Model (LLM) agents have demonstrated remarkable capabilities in reasoning and tool use, yet they often suffer from rigid, reactive control flows that limit their adaptability and efficiency. Most existing frameworks rely on fixed pipelines or failure-triggered reflection, causing agents to act impulsively or correct errors only after they occur. In this paper, we introduce Heartbeat-Driven Autonomous Thinking Activity Scheduling, a mechanism that enables proactive, adaptive, and continuous self-regulation. Mirroring the natural rhythm of human cognition, our system employs a periodic ``heartbeat'' mechanism to orchestrate a dynamic repertoire of cognitive modules (e.g., Planner, Critic, Recaller, Dreamer). Unlike traditional approaches that rely on hard-coded symbolic rules or immediate reactive triggers, our scheduler learns to determine when to engage specific thinking activities -- such as recalling memories, summarizing experiences, or strategic planning -- based on temporal patterns and historical context. This functional approach allows cognitive modules to be dynamically added or removed without structural reengineering. Meanwhile, we propose a meta-learning strategy for continual policy adaptation, where the scheduler optimizes its cognitive strategy over time using historical interaction logs. Evaluation results demonstrate that our approach effectively learns to schedule cognitive activities based on historical data and can autonomously integrate new thinking modules.

</details>


### [99] [Fun-TSG: A Function-Driven Multivariate Time Series Generator with Variable-Level Anomaly Labeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14221)
*Pierre Lotte,André Péninou,Olivier Teste*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Fun-TSG是一个用于评估多元时间序列异常检测方法的完全可定制的时间序列生成器，解决现有基准数据集缺乏细粒度标注、依赖关系不明确等问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前多元时间序列异常检测基准数据集存在三大局限：缺乏细粒度异常标注、无法提供明确的变量间和时序依赖关系、对数据生成机制缺乏透明度。这严重阻碍了检测模型的发展和严格比较，尤其是针对可解释和变量级别输出的模型。

Method: Fun-TSG支持两种生成模式：(1) 全自动化生成——基于随机采样的依赖结构和异常类型；(2) 手动生成——通过用户自定义方程和异常配置。两种模式均提供完整的数据生成过程透明度，包括变量级和时间戳级的真值异常标签。

Result: 该工具能够创建多样化、可解释、可复现的基准测试场景，支持对经典和现代异常检测模型进行细粒度的性能分析。

Conclusion: Fun-TSG填补了异常检测评估工具的空白，为研究者和实践者提供了灵活、透明、高质量的基准测试生成能力，有助于推动异常检测技术的标准化评估和模型改进。

Abstract: Reliable evaluation of anomaly detection methods in multivariate time series remains an open challenge, largely due to the limitations of existing benchmark datasets. Current resources often lack fine-grained anomaly annotations, do not provide explicit intervariable and temporal dependencies, and offer little insight into the underlying generative mechanisms. These shortcomings hinder the development and rigorous comparison of detection models, especially those targeting interpretable and variable-specific outputs. To address this gap, we introduce Fun-TSG, a fully customizable time series generator designed to support high-quality evaluation of anomaly detection systems. Our tool enables both fully automated generation, based on randomly sampled dependency structures and anomaly types, and manual generation through user-defined equations and anomaly configurations. In both cases, it provides full transparency over the data generation process, including access to ground-truth anomaly labels at the variable and timestamp levels. Fun-TSG supports the creation of diverse, interpretable, and reproducible benchmarking scenarios, enabling fine-grained performance analysis for both classical and modern anomaly detection models.

</details>


### [100] [CAMO: An Agentic Framework for Automated Causal Discovery from Micro Behaviors to Macro Emergence in LLM Agent Simulations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14691)
*Xiangning Yu,Yuwei Guo,Yuqi Hou,Xiao Xue,Qun Ma*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: CAMO是一个自动化的因果发现框架，旨在从LLM智能体模拟中揭示微观行为到宏观涌现的因果机制。该框架将机制假设转化为可计算因子，学习以涌现目标Y为中心的紧凑因果表示，输出可计算的马尔可夫边界和最小上游解释子图，并利用模拟器内部反事实探测来定向模糊边和修正假设。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前LLM智能体模拟被广泛用于研究社会涌现现象，但宏观结果背后的微观到宏观因果机制往往不清楚。这是因为涌现源于智能体之间相互交织的互动以及中观层面的反馈和非线性，使得生成机制难以解耦。

Method: CAMO将机制假设转化为基于模拟记录的可计算因子，学习以涌现目标Y为中心的紧凑因果表示；输出可计算的马尔可夫边界和最小上游解释子图，生成可解释的因果链和可操作的干预杠杆；利用模拟器内部反事实探测来定向模糊边，并在证据与当前认知矛盾时修正假设。

Result: 在四个涌现场景下的实验证明了CAMO的有效性。

Conclusion: CAMO能够有效地从LLM智能体模拟中揭示微观行为到宏观涌现的因果机制，为理解和干预复杂系统中的涌现现象提供了有价值的工具。

Abstract: LLM-empowered agent simulations are increasingly used to study social emergence, yet the micro-to-macro causal mechanisms behind macro outcomes often remain unclear. This is challenging because emergence arises from intertwined agent interactions and meso-level feedback and nonlinearity, making generative mechanisms hard to disentangle. To this end, we introduce \textbf{\textsc{CAMO}}, an automated \textbf{Ca}usal discovery framework from \textbf{M}icr\textbf{o} behaviors to \textbf{M}acr\textbf{o} Emergence in LLM agent simulations. \textsc{CAMO} converts mechanistic hypotheses into computable factors grounded in simulation records and learns a compact causal representation centered on an emergent target $Y$. \textsc{CAMO} outputs a computable Markov boundary and a minimal upstream explanatory subgraph, yielding interpretable causal chains and actionable intervention levers. It also uses simulator-internal counterfactual probing to orient ambiguous edges and revise hypotheses when evidence contradicts the current view. Experiments across four emergent settings demonstrate the promise of \textsc{CAMO}.

</details>


### [101] [Layered Mutability: Continuity and Governance in Persistent Self-Modifying Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14717)
*Krti Tallam*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: Persistent language-model agents increasingly combine tool use, tiered memory, reflective prompting, and runtime adaptation. In such systems, behavior is shaped not only by current prompts but by mutable internal conditions that influence future action. This paper introduces layered mutability, a framework for reasoning about that process across five layers: pretraining, post-training alignment, self-narrative, memory, and weight-level adaptation. The central claim is that governance difficulty rises when mutation is rapid, downstream coupling is strong, reversibility is weak, and observability is low, creating a systematic mismatch between the layers that most affect behavior and the layers humans can most easily inspect. I formalize this intuition with simple drift, governance-load, and hysteresis quantities, connect the framework to recent work on temporal identity in language-model agents, and report a preliminary ratchet experiment in which reverting an agent's visible self-description after memory accumulation fails to restore baseline behavior. In that experiment, the estimated identity hysteresis ratio is 0.68. The main implication is that the salient failure mode for persistent self-modifying agents is not abrupt misalignment but compositional drift: locally reasonable updates that accumulate into a behavioral trajectory that was never explicitly authorized.

</details>


### [102] [Formalizing Kantian Ethics: Formula of the Universal Law Logic (FULL)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14254)
*Taylor Olson*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: The field of machine ethics aims to build Artificial Moral Agents (AMAs) to better understand morality and make AI agents safer. To do so, many approaches encode human moral intuition as a set of axioms on actions e.g., do not harm, you must help others. However, this introduces (at least) two limitations for future AMAs. First, it does not consider the agent's purposes in performing the action. Second, it assumes that we humans can enumerate our moral intuition. This paper explores formalizing a moral procedure that alleviates these two limitations. We specifically consider Kantian ethics and present a multi-sorted quantified modal logic we call the Formula of the Universal Law Logic (FULL). The FULL formalizes Kant's first formulation of the categorical imperative, the Formula of the Universal Law (FUL), and concepts such as causality and agency. We demonstrate on three cases from Kantian ethics that the FULL can reason to evaluate agents' actions for certain purposes without built-in moral intuition, given that it has sufficient (non-normative) background knowledge. Therefore, the FULL is a contribution towards more robust and autonomous AMAs, and a more formal understanding of Kantian ethics.

</details>


### [103] [The Missing Knowledge Layer in AI: A Framework for Stable Human-AI Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14881)
*Rikard Rosenbacke,Carl Rosenbacke,Victor Rosenbacke,Martin McKee*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 五篇论文系列的第一篇，提出双层方法来稳定人机推理：人类侧机制（不确定性提示、冲突显现、可追溯推理痕迹）和模型侧认知控制循环（ECL），使不确定性和漂移可见以实现更精准的能力治理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在医疗、法律、金融等高风险领域被广泛使用，但存在关键缺陷：流畅的输出可能掩盖不确定性和推理漂移。人类也容易将流畅性误认为可靠性。这导致AI在关键场景中既不可靠也无法治理，需要建立 stabilizing human-AI reasoning 的操作基础。

Method: 提出双层方法：(1) 人类侧机制（论文II-IV）：不确定性提示、冲突显现、可追溯推理痕迹；(2) 模型侧机制（论文V）：认知控制循环（ECL），检测推理不稳定性并调节生成过程。

Result: 该框架在用户交互点增加信噪比，使不确定性和漂移在治理执行前可见，实现实时条件下的可追溯推理过程，支持EU AI Act和ISO/IEC 42001合规要求。

Conclusion: 流畅性≠可靠性。在缺乏稳定人类和模型推理结构的情况下，AI无法在关键领域被信任或有效治理。该系列工作为能力治理提供了缺失的操作基础。

Abstract: Large language models are increasingly integrated into decision-making in areas such as healthcare, law, finance, engineering, and government. Yet they share a critical limitation: they produce fluent outputs even when their internal reasoning has drifted. A confident answer can conceal uncertainty, speculation, or inconsistency, and small changes in phrasing can lead to different conclusions. This makes LLMs useful assistants but unreliable partners in high-stakes contexts.
  Humans exhibit a similar weakness, often mistaking fluency for reliability. When a model responds smoothly, users tend to trust it, even when both model and user are drifting together. This paper is the first in a five-paper research series on stabilising human-AI reasoning. The series proposes a two-layer approach: Parts II-IV introduce human-side mechanisms such as uncertainty cues, conflict surfacing, and auditable reasoning traces, while Part V develops a model-side Epistemic Control Loop (ECL) that detects instability and modulates generation accordingly.
  Together, these layers form a missing operational substrate for governance by increasing signal-to-noise at the point of use. Stabilising interaction makes uncertainty and drift visible before enforcement is applied, enabling more precise capability governance. This aligns with emerging compliance expectations, including the EU AI Act and ISO/IEC 42001, by making reasoning processes traceable under real conditions of use.
  The central claim is that fluency is not reliability. Without structures that stabilise both human and model reasoning, AI cannot be trusted or governed where it matters most.

</details>


### [104] [Credo: Declarative Control of LLM Pipelines via Beliefs and Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14401)
*Duo Lu,Andrew Crotty,Uğur Çetintemel*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Credo框架通过将语义状态表示为"信念(beliefs)"并使用声明式策略规范行为，解决了现有AI智能体系统的脆弱性、不透明性和难验证性问题。该框架通过数据库支撑的语义控制平面实现自适应、可审计和可组合的执行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI智能体框架依赖命令式控制循环、临时记忆和嵌入提示的逻辑，导致行为不透明、脆弱且难以验证。随着AI系统在需要长期、状态化决策的领域中的应用日益普及，亟需一种更可靠的行为调控机制。

Method: 提出Credo框架，核心设计包括：(1) 将语义状态表示为"信念"(beliefs)；(2) 定义声明式策略(declarative policies)对信念进行约束；(3) 采用数据库支撑的语义控制平面实现执行管理。

Result: 该设计支持自适应、审计友好和可组合的执行流程。在决策控制场景中验证，信念和策略可声明式地引导关键执行决策（如模型选择、检索、纠正性重执行），无需修改底层流水线代码即可实现动态行为调整。

Conclusion: Credo证明了将语义状态抽象为信念并结合声明式策略是实现可控、可验证AI智能体的有效路径，为构建下一代可靠的多模型协作系统提供了架构基础。

Abstract: Agentic AI systems are becoming commonplace in domains that require long-lived, stateful decision-making in continuously evolving conditions. As such, correctness depends not only on the output of individual model calls, but also on how to best adapt when incorporating new evidence or revising prior conclusions. However, existing frameworks rely on imperative control loops, ephemeral memory, and prompt-embedded logic, making agent behavior opaque, brittle, and difficult to verify. This paper introduces Credo, which represents semantic state as beliefs and regulates behavior using declarative policies defined over these beliefs. This design supports adaptive, auditable, and composable execution through a database-backed semantic control plane. We showcase these concepts in a decision-control scenario, where beliefs and policies declaratively guide critical execution choices (e.g., model selection, retrieval, corrective re-execution), enabling dynamic behavior without requiring any changes to the underlying pipeline code.

</details>


### [105] [GFT: From Imitation to Reward Fine-Tuning with Unbiased Group Advantages and Dynamic Coefficient Rectification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14258)
*Wangjie Gan,Miao Pan,Linbo Xi,Wenqi Zhang,Jintao Chen,Jianwei Yin,Xuhong Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过训练动态分析揭示了SFT存在稀疏隐式奖励、不稳定逆概率加权等问题，提出Group Fine-Tuning (GFT)统一后训练框架，通过组优势学习和动态系数校正机制，在知识注入和泛化能力上优于传统SFT方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型后训练中，如何将高效知识注入与鲁棒泛化能力统一起来仍具挑战。SFT作为常用的后训练方法，其内在局限性尚未被充分诊断和解决。

Method: 1) 训练动态分析：将SFT解释为策略梯度优化的特殊形式，识别出稀疏隐式奖励和不稳定逆概率加权两个核心问题；2) Group Advantage Learning：构建多样化响应组，推导归一化对比监督信号以缓解奖励稀疏；3) Dynamic Coefficient Rectification：自适应约束逆概率权重以稳定优化过程。

Result: GFT一致性地超越基于SFT的方法，产生的策略与后续RL训练的结合更加平滑。

Conclusion: GFT通过组学习机制和动态系数校正，有效解决了SFT的内在局限性，为大语言模型后训练提供了统一且更优的框架。

Abstract: Large language models are typically post-trained using supervised fine-tuning (SFT) and reinforcement learning (RL), yet effectively unifying efficient knowledge injection with robust generalization remains challenging. In this work, we provide a training-dynamics analysis showing that SFT can be interpreted as a special case of policy gradient optimization with an extremely sparse implicit reward and unstable inverse-probability weighting, which together lead to single-path dependency, entropy collapse, and gradient explosion. Motivated by this diagnosis, we propose Group Fine-Tuning (GFT), a unified post-training framework that addresses these intrinsic limitations through two mechanisms: Group Advantage Learning, which constructs diverse response groups and derives normalized contrastive supervision to alleviate reward sparsity, and Dynamic Coefficient Rectification, which adaptively bounds inverse-probability weights to stabilize optimization while preserving efficient knowledge injection. Experiments demonstrate that GFT consistently surpasses SFT-based methods and yields policies that integrate more smoothly with subsequent RL training.

</details>


### [106] [Governing Reflective Human-AI Collaboration: A Framework for Epistemic Scaffolding and Traceable Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14898)
*Rikard Rosenbacke,Carl Rosenbacke,Victor Rosenbacke,Martin McKee*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出将推理从模型内部能力重新定位到人机交互层面。通过"The Architect's Pen"方法，将人机对话本身构建为一个推理循环：人类抽象→模型表达→人类反思。核心贡献在于将System-2推理从模型工程问题转化为认知协议设计问题，为可审计、可解释的AI应用提供了无需新模型架构的实践路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前大语言模型虽能生成流畅输出，但缺乏时间连续性、因果反馈和现实世界锚定。其"反思"仅为语言模拟而非真正的理解性推理。传统方法试图在模型内部工程化推理能力，但这条路存在根本局限。

Method: 1) 将推理重新定义为分布式认知过程，置于人机交互层而非模型内部；2) 提出"The Architect's Pen"方法，将AI作为外部媒介用于结构化反思，对话过程为：人类抽象→模型表达→人类反思；3) 通过可测量的交互协议实现推理的可审计性与治理合规。

Result: 该框架支持推理痕迹的可追溯性，并可与EU AI Act、ISO/IEC 42001等新兴治理标准对齐。

Conclusion: 本文将问题核心从"模型是否能思考"重构为"人机系统是否能推理"。该框架提供了一条无需新模型架构即可实现更透明、可控、可问责AI使用的实践路径，强调协作智能（人类判断+情境理解+机器速度+记忆+联想能力）。
