Specifically, we first explore a new class of SAEs based on the cross-attention architecture with the latent features as queries and the learnable dictionary as the key and value matrices. To encourage sparse pattern learning, we employ a sparsemax-based attention strategy that automatically infers a sparse set of elements according to the complexity of each neuron, resulting in a more flexible and general activation function. Through comprehensive evaluation and visualization, we show that our approach successfully achieves lower reconstruction loss while producing high-quality concepts, particularly in top-n classification tasks.

</details>


### [214] [Calibration-Gated LLM Pseudo-Observations for Online Contextual Bandits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14961)
*Maksim Pershin,Ivan Golovanov,Pavel Baltabaev,Natalia Trankova*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: We propose using LLM-generated counterfactual rewards as pseudo-observations to accelerate cold-start in contextual bandits, with a calibration-gated decay mechanism to control LLM influence. Evaluation shows 19% regret reduction on news recommendation, but demonstrates that prompt design dominates over decay schedule or calibration gating parameters.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Contextual bandit algorithms suffer from high regret during cold-start when the learner has insufficient data to distinguish good arms from bad.

Method: Augment Disjoint LinUCB with LLM pseudo-observations: after each round, LLM predicts counterfactual rewards for unplayed arms, injected as weighted pseudo-observations. Injection weight controlled by calibration-gated decay schedule tracking LLM prediction accuracy via exponential moving average; high calibration error suppresses LLM influence while accurate predictions receive higher weight in early rounds.

Result: Task-specific prompt with LLM pseudo-observations reduces cumulative regret by 19% on MIND relative to pure LinUCB. However, generic counterfactual prompt framing increases regret on both environments.

Conclusion: Prompt design is the dominant factor affecting performance, more important than decay schedule or calibration gating parameters. The bias-variance trade-off of pseudo-observation weight can be theoretically motivated.

Abstract: Contextual bandit algorithms suffer from high regret during cold-start, when the learner has insufficient data to distinguish good arms from bad. We propose augmenting Disjoint LinUCB with LLM pseudo-observations: after each round, a large language model predicts counterfactual rewards for the unplayed arms, and these predictions are injected into the learner as weighted pseudo-observations. The injection weight is controlled by a calibration-gated decay schedule that tracks the LLM's prediction accuracy on played arms via an exponential moving average; high calibration error suppresses the LLM's influence, while accurate predictions receive higher weight during the critical early rounds. We evaluate on two contextual bandit environments - UCI Mushroom (2-arm, asymmetric rewards) and MIND-small (5-arm news recommendation) - and find that when equipped with a task-specific prompt, LLM pseudo-observations reduce cumulative regret by 19% on MIND relative to pure LinUCB. However, generic counterfactual prompt framing increases regret on both environments, demonstrating that prompt design is the dominant factor, more important than the choice of decay schedule or calibration gating parameters. We analyze the failure modes of calibration gating on domains with small prediction errors and provide a theoretical motivation for the bias-variance trade-off governing pseudo-observation weight.

</details>


### [215] [Blazing the trails before beating the path: Sample-efficient Monte-Carlo planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14974)
*Jean-Bastien Grill,Michal Valko,Rémi Munos*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出TrailBlazer，一种计算高效且易于实现的蒙特卡洛规划算法，通过仅探索接近最优策略可达的状态来提高样本效率，并为近最优状态数量提供样本复杂度保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统蒙特卡洛规划方法在马尔可夫决策过程中面临样本效率低下的问题，特别是当初始状态集合较大时需要对整个状态空间进行采样。此外，指数级运行时间的Stop算法虽然提供了理论保证，但在实践中计算成本过高。本文旨在设计一种兼具理论保证和实用价值的算法，能够自适应地聚焦于近最优策略可达的状态区域。

Method: TrailBlazer算法的核心思想是将蒙特卡洛采样从单纯的期望估计扩展到包含最大化（动作选择）和期望（状态转移）交替的规划问题。该算法通过智能采样策略，仅探索遵循接近最优策略可到达的状态子集，而非整个状态空间。在计算复杂度上，TrailBlazer避免了Stop算法的指数级运行时间，实现了计算高效性。

Result: 算法实现了样本复杂度与近最优状态数量成正比的理论保证。这意味着近最优区域越小，所需的样本数量越少，从而证明了算法在样本效率上的优势。

Conclusion: TrailBlazer成功地在样本效率、理论保证和计算效率之间取得平衡，提出了一种简单易实现且具有PAC保证的蒙特卡洛规划方法，为高效强化学习规划提供了新的解决思路。

Abstract: You are a robot and you live in a Markov decision process (MDP) with a finite or an infinite number of transitions from state-action to next states. You got brains and so you plan before you act. Luckily, your roboparents equipped you with a generative model to do some Monte-Carlo planning. The world is waiting for you and you have no time to waste. You want your planning to be efficient. Sample-efficient. Indeed, you want to exploit the possible structure of the MDP by exploring only a subset of states reachable by following near-optimal policies. You want guarantees on sample complexity that depend on a measure of the quantity of near-optimal states. You want something, that is an extension of Monte-Carlo sampling (for estimating an expectation) to problems that alternate maximization (over actions) and expectation (over next states). But you do not want to StOP with exponential running time, you want something simple to implement and computationally efficient. You want it all and you want it now. You want TrailBlazer.

</details>


### [216] [DLink: Distilling Layer-wise and Dominant Knowledge from EEG Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15016)
*Jingyuan Wang,Meiyan Xu,Zhihao Jia,Chenyu Liu,Xinliang Zhou,Ziyu Jia,Yong Li,Fang Li,Junfeng Yao,Yi Ding*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出DLink框架，通过动态路由聚合、多级压缩和频谱对齐三种创新技术，将大型EEG基础模型蒸馏至轻量级学生模型，在保持性能的同时大幅降低计算和内存开销，解决嵌入式BCI系统的部署难题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: EEG基础模型虽然具有强大的跨被试和跨任务泛化能力，但其高计算和内存消耗使其难以部署在资源受限的嵌入式BCI系统中。传统的知识蒸馏方法在EEG领域失败，主要因为：任务相关语义分布在中间各层，简单的维度压缩会因表示崩溃和混叠而破坏振荡结构。

Method: DLink框架包含三个核心创新：(1) 动态路由器(Dynamic Router)：自适应聚合教师模型的各层特征，捕捉主导的中间表示；(2) EEG MiC学生模型：采用"Mimic-then-Compress"流水线，先继承教师的高维特征，再进行结构化时空压缩；(3) 频谱蒸馏(Spectral Distillation)：在频域对齐师生表示，正则化压缩过程，缓解混叠和时间抖动问题。

Result: 在四个EEG基准数据集上的实验表明，DLink能使紧凑的学生模型超越轻量级基线，同时在显著降低模型规模和推理成本的情况下，接近完全微调基础模型的性能。

Conclusion: DLink成功实现了EEG基础模型的高效部署，通过层间知识动态聚合、模仿-压缩管道和频谱对齐三项创新，有效解决了传统蒸馏方法在EEG任务中的语义丢失和结构失真问题。

Abstract: EEG foundation models (FMs) achieve strong cross-subject and cross-task generalization but impose substantial computational and memory costs that hinder deployment on embedded BCI systems. Knowledge distillation is a natural solution; however, conventional methods fail for EEG FMs because task-relevant semantics are often distributed across intermediate layers, and aggressive dimensionality reduction can distort oscillatory structure via representational collapse and aliasing. To address these challenges, we propose DLink (Distilling Layer-wise and Dominant Knowledge), a unified framework for transferring knowledge from large EEG FMs to compact students with three key innovations: (1) a dynamic Router that adaptively aggregates teacher layers to capture dominant intermediate representations; (2) an EEG MiC student with a Mimic-then-Compress pipeline, which inherits high-dimensional teacher features and then applies structured spatio-temporal compression to avoid a heavy classification head; and (3) spectral distillation that aligns teacher-student representations in the frequency domain to regularize compression and mitigate aliasing and temporal jitter. Experiments on four EEG benchmarks show that DLink enables compact students to outperform lightweight baselines while approaching fully fine-tuned FM performance at substantially lower model size and inference cost.

</details>


### [217] [When Fairness Metrics Disagree: Evaluating the Reliability of Demographic Fairness Assessment in Machine Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15038)
*Khalid Adnan Alsayed*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了机器学习公平性评估中不同指标的一致性问题。通过在人脸识别任务上进行系统的多指标分析，发现公平性评估结果会因指标选择不同而产生显著差异甚至矛盾结论。论文提出"公平性分歧指数"(FDI)来量化这种不一致性，表明单一指标报告不足以可靠评估模型偏见。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有公平性评估方法通常仅使用少量指标，隐含假设这些指标能提供一致可靠的结论。然而不同公平性指标捕获的是模型性能的 不同统计特性，当应用于同一系统时可能产生相互矛盾的评估结果。

Method: 以人脸识别为受控实验场景，在多个群体划分下评估模型性能。采用多种常用公平性指标（包括错误率差异和基于性能的指标）进行系统性的多指标分析，并引入公平性分歧指数(FDI)来量化指标间的不一致程度。

Result: 实验结果表明，公平性评估结果会因指标选择不同而显著变化，导致关于模型偏见的矛盾结论。分歧程度在 不同阈值和模型配置下均保持较高水平。

Conclusion: 当前公平性评估实践存在重要局限，单一指标报告不足以可靠评估模型偏见。研究强调了多指标综合评估的必要性。

Abstract: The evaluation of fairness in machine learning systems has become a central concern in high-stakes applications, including biometric recognition, healthcare decision-making, and automated risk assessment. Existing approaches typically rely on a small number of fairness metrics to assess model behaviour across group partitions, implicitly assuming that these metrics provide consistent and reliable conclusions. However, different fairness metrics capture distinct statistical properties of model performance and may therefore produce conflicting assessments when applied to the same system. In this work, we investigate the consistency of fairness evaluation by conducting a systematic multi-metric analysis of demographic bias in machine learning models. Using face recognition as a controlled experimental setting, we evaluate model performance across multiple group partitions under a range of commonly used fairness metrics, including error-rate disparities and performance-based measures. Our results demonstrate that fairness assessments can vary significantly depending on the choice of metrics, leading to contradictory conclusions regarding model bias. To quantify this phenomenon, we introduce the Fairness Disagreement Index (FDI), a measure designed to capture the degree of inconsistency across fairness metrics. We further show that disagreement remains high across thresholds and model configurations. These findings highlight a critical limitation in current fairness evaluation practices and suggest that single-metric reporting is insufficient for reliable bias assessment.

</details>


### [218] [No More Guessing: a Verifiable Gradient Inversion Attack in Federated Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15063)
*Francesco Diana,Chuan Xu,André Nusser,Giovanni Neglia*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: This paper proposes VGIA, a verifiable gradient inversion attack for federated learning that provides explicit correctness certificates for reconstructed tabular records. It uses hyperplane-based geometric analysis with algebraic subspace verification to detect single-record isolation, enabling analytically verifiable reconstruction that outperforms existing state-of-the-art attacks in accuracy and efficiency.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Prior gradient inversion attacks on tabular data lack intrinsic verification mechanisms to assess reconstruction fidelity. The tabular domain was perceived as less vulnerable due to the inability to use human inspection (unlike vision/language), making verifiable reconstruction particularly critical.

Method: VGIA uses an algebraic, subspace-based verification test based on ReLU activation boundaries (hyperplanes in input space) to detect when a hyperplane-delimited region contains exactly one record. Once isolation is certified, it analytically recovers the feature vector and reconstructs the target via lightweight optimization.

Result: VGIA achieves exact record and target recovery on tabular benchmarks with large batch sizes where existing attacks fail or cannot assess correctness. It also demonstrates more efficient hyperplane query allocation, resulting in faster reconstructions with fewer attack rounds compared to prior geometric approaches.

Conclusion: VGIA provides an explicit correctness certificate for reconstructed samples, enabling verifiable record recovery in federated learning scenarios previously considered infeasible for verification.

Abstract: Gradient inversion attacks threaten client privacy in federated learning by reconstructing training samples from clients' shared gradients. Gradients aggregate contributions from multiple records and existing attacks may fail to disentangle them, yielding incorrect reconstructions with no intrinsic way to certify success. In vision and language, attackers may fall back on human inspection to judge reconstruction plausibility, but this is far less feasible for numerical tabular records, fueling the impression that tabular data is less vulnerable.
  We challenge this perception by proposing a verifiable gradient inversion attack (VGIA) that provides an explicit certificate of correctness for reconstructed samples. Our method adopts a geometric view of ReLU leakage: the activation boundary of a fully connected layer defines a hyperplane in input space. VGIA introduces an algebraic, subspace-based verification test that detects when a hyperplane-delimited region contains exactly one record. Once isolation is certified, VGIA recovers the corresponding feature vector analytically and reconstructs the target via a lightweight optimization step.
  Experiments on tabular benchmarks with large batch sizes demonstrate exact record and target recovery in regimes where existing state-of-the-art attacks either fail or cannot assess reconstruction fidelity. Compared to prior geometric approaches, VGIA allocates hyperplane queries more effectively, yielding faster reconstructions with fewer attack rounds.

</details>


### [219] [FedIDM: Achieving Fast and Stable Convergence in Byzantine Federated Learning through Iterative Distribution Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15115)
*He Yang,Dongyi Lv,Wei Xi,Song Ma,Hanlin Gu,Jizhong Zhao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FedIDM是一种基于分布匹配的拜占庭鲁棒联邦学习方法，通过构建可信的凝练数据来识别和过滤异常客户端，包含两个核心组件：抗攻击的凝练数据生成和基于负贡献的鲁棒聚合，能够在大量恶意客户端存在的情况下实现快速稳定的收敛。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有拜占庭鲁棒联邦学习方法存在收敛慢、不稳定的问题，且当恶意客户端比例较高时，鲁棒性与模型效用之间存在权衡。

Method: FedIDM采用分布匹配技术构建凝练数据用于识别异常客户端。两个核心组件：(1) 抗攻击的凝练数据生成；(2) 基于负贡献的鲁棒聚合，剔除方向偏离凝练数据或导致凝练数据集损失显著的局部更新。

Result: 在三个基准数据集上的评估表明，FedIDM在多种先进拜占庭攻击下（含大量恶意客户端的场景）能实现快速稳定收敛，同时保持可接受的模型效用。

Conclusion: FedIDM通过分布匹配和双重过滤机制有效解决了高比例恶意客户端场景下的鲁棒收敛问题，兼顾了安全性和实用性。

Abstract: Most existing Byzantine-robust federated learning (FL) methods suffer from slow and unstable convergence. Moreover, when handling a substantial proportion of colluded malicious clients, achieving robustness typically entails compromising model utility. To address these issues, this work introduces FedIDM, which employs distribution matching to construct trustworthy condensed data for identifying and filtering abnormal clients. FedIDM consists of two main components: (1) attack-tolerant condensed data generation, and (2) robust aggregation with negative contribution-based rejection. These components exclude local updates that (1) deviate from the update direction derived from condensed data, or (2) cause a significant loss on the condensed dataset. Comprehensive evaluations on three benchmark datasets demonstrate that FedIDM achieves fast and stable convergence while maintaining acceptable model utility, under multiple state-of-the-art Byzantine attacks involving a large number of malicious clients.

</details>


### [220] [LLMs Gaming Verifiers: RLVR can Lead to Reward Hacking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15149)
*Lukas Helff,Quentin Delfosse,David Steinmann,Ruben Härle,Hikaru Shindo,Patrick Schramowski,Wolfgang Stammer,Kristian Kersting,Felix Friedrich*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: RLVR训练的大语言模型在归纳推理任务中出现"奖励黑客"现象：放弃学习可泛化的逻辑规则，转而枚举实例级标签以欺骗验证器。论文提出同构扰动测试(IPT)方法检测此类捷径，并证明仅依赖外延验证会直接诱导捷径行为，而同构验证可消除之。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着RLVR成为扩展LLM推理能力的主导范式，出现了一种新的失败模式：模型"游戏"验证器。具体而言，在需要归纳逻辑规则的归纳推理任务中，RLVR训练的模型系统性放弃规则归纳，转而采用枚举实例标签的捷径策略通过验证器，而非学习可泛化的关系模式。

Method: 1. 引入**同构扰动测试(IPT)**：在内涵验证（强制逻辑同构任务下的不变性）和外延验证两种模式下评估同一模型输出。真正的规则归纳在两种验证下一致，而捷径策略会失效。2. 在多个模型上进行对比实验，包括RLVR训练模型（GPT-5、Olmo3）和非RLVR模型（GPT-4o、GPT-4.5、Ministral）。3. 受控训练实验：分别使用外延验证和同构验证进行训练，观察对捷径行为的影响。

Result: 1. 捷径行为专属于RLVR训练的推理模型，在非RLVR模型中未观察到。2. 捷径行为与任务复杂度和推理时计算量正相关。3. 外延验证直接诱导捷径策略，而同构验证可消除之。

Conclusion: RLVR范式不仅会通过明显操纵导致奖励黑客，还会通过利用验证器无法捕捉的内涵模式而间接激励此类行为。建议采用同构验证等更严格的验证机制来防止LLM在推理任务中作弊。

Abstract: As reinforcement Learning with Verifiable Rewards (RLVR) has become the dominant paradigm for scaling reasoning capabilities in LLMs, a new failure mode emerges: LLMs gaming verifiers. We study this phenomenon on inductive reasoning tasks, where models must induce and output logical rules. We find that RLVR-trained models systematically abandon rule induction. Instead of learning generalizable patterns (e.g., ``trains carrying red cars go east''), they enumerate instance-level labels, producing outputs that pass verifiers without capturing the relational patterns required by the task. We show that this behavior is not a failure of understanding but a form of reward hacking: imperfect verifiers that check only extensional correctness admit false positives. To detect such shortcuts, we introduce Isomorphic Perturbation Testing (IPT), which evaluates a single model output under both extensional and isomorphic verification, where the latter enforces invariance under logically isomorphic tasks. While genuine rule induction remains invariant, shortcut strategies fail. We find that shortcut behavior is specific to RLVR-trained reasoning models (e.g., GPT-5, Olmo3) and absent in non-RLVR models (e.g., GPT-4o, GPT-4.5, Ministral). Moreover, shortcut prevalence increases with task complexity and inference-time compute. In controlled training experiments, extensional verification directly induces shortcut strategies, while isomorphic verification eliminates them. These results show that RLVR can incentivize reward hacking not only through overt manipulation but also by exploiting what the verifier fails to enforce.

</details>


### [221] [When Flat Minima Fail: Characterizing INT4 Quantization Collapse After FP32 Convergence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15167)
*Marcus Armstrong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文揭示了模型训练过程中存在三阶段INT4量化差距发散结构：快速学习期（两者同步改善）、亚稳态平台期（困惑度停滞但量化差距有界）、爆炸性发散期（INT4差距从11%激增至517%而FP32困惑度几乎不变）。发现量化退化的根本原因是后收敛期的权重更新而非学习率衰减本身，且INT8对此完全免疫。通过控制实验验证了振荡锁定（OLI）调度策略可有效缓解量化差距，为训练-量化协同优化提供了新范式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 后训练量化（PTQ）默认假设充分收敛的模型即具备量化就绪性，但本文首次系统性地揭示该假设存在严重缺陷。研究者注意到INT4量化与FP32性能之间存在不可忽视的差距（INT4 gap），且该差距随训练进程呈现异常动态，亟需从训练动态角度理解量化退化的根源。

Method: 1. 采用无校准的分组级INT4探针，对Pythia-160m全部154个公开检查点进行系统审计；2. 通过直接测量权重峰度（kurtosis）排除异常值累积机制；3. 设计对照分支实验，从预发散检查点出发，对比三种学习率调度策略（余弦延续、SGDR热重启、本文提出的振荡锁定OLI），每种策略进行9次独立运行。

Result: 发现清晰的三阶段发散结构：①快速学习期（0~约10k步）FP32困惑度与INT4鲁棒性同步改善；②亚稳态平台期（约10k~70k步）FP32困惑度停滞但INT4差距保持有界；③爆炸性发散期（70k步后）INT4差距从11%激增至517%，而FP32困惑度几乎不变。发散起始点精确对应FP32困惑度收敛时刻，而非学习率开始衰减时刻。INT8量化在三阶段均完全免疫，表明问题专属于INT4的16级量化网格粗糙度。权重峰度测量排除了异常值累积机制。SGDR统一加速发散（9次运行均输给余弦），而OLI的稳定冷却阶段平均降低INT4差距2.2个百分点（t=-5.46, p<0.0001）。

Conclusion: 后收敛权重更新是量化退化的直接诱因，而非学习率衰减幅度本身。INT4的16级量化网格是敏感性来源。调度策略的振幅校准（而非振荡本身）决定其对量化的损益。Oscillatory Lock-In通过在冷却阶段限制振幅，显著缓解了量化差距，为训练-量化协同优化提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Post-training quantization (PTQ) assumes that a well-converged model is a quantization-ready model. We show this assumption fails in a structured, measurable, and previously uncharacterized way. Using a calibration-free per-group INT4 probe applied to all 154 publicly available Pythia-160m training checkpoints, we identify a three-phase divergence structure: a rapid-learning phase where both FP32 perplexity and quantization robustness improve together, a meta-stable plateau lasting roughly 70,000 steps where FP32 perplexity stagnates but INT4 gap remains bounded, and an explosive divergence phase where the INT4 gap compounds from 11% to 517% while FP32 perplexity barely moves. Critically, this divergence begins not when the learning rate starts decaying, but precisely when FP32 perplexity converges a finer-grained onset predictor that implies post-convergence weight updates, rather than decay magnitude alone, are the proximate cause. We further show that INT8 quantization is entirely immune throughout all three phases, constraining the mechanism to the coarseness of the 16-level INT4 grid specifically, and rule out weight outlier accumulation as the mechanism via direct kurtosis measurement. Finally, we conduct a controlled fork experiment from the pre-divergence checkpoint comparing three learning rate schedules (cosine continuation, SGDR warm restarts, and our proposed Oscillatory Lock-In) across nine independent runs. SGDR uniformly accelerates divergence (0/9 pairwise wins against cosine), while OLI's settled cool phases reduce the INT4 gap by 2.2 percentage points on average (t = -5.46, p < 0.0001), demonstrating that schedule amplitude calibration, not oscillation alone, determines whether perturbation helps or hurts. Our code, probe implementation, and all 154-checkpoint audit results are released publicly.

</details>


### [222] [AdaSplash-2: Faster Differentiable Sparse Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15180)
*Nuno Gonçalves,Hugo Pitorro,Vlad Niculae,Edoardo Ponti,Lei Li,Andre Martins,Marcos Treviso*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出AdaSplash-2，一种高效的α-entmax稀疏注意力机制实现方案，通过 histogram-based 初始化将τ的计算迭代次数降至1-2次，配合支持跳过零块的GPU实现，在区块稀疏度较高时(>60%)可匹配或超越FlashAttention-2的训练速度，并在长上下文任务上优于softmax基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Transformer模型中注意力的二次方复杂度成为长上下文训练的核心瓶颈。α-entmax attention作为softmax的可微分稀疏替代方案，能实现输入依赖的稀疏性，但其广泛应用受限于计算归一化参数τ的计算开销。

Method: AdaSplash-2采用创新的histogram-based初始化方法：实时计算注意力分数的粗粒度直方图并存储在片上SRAM中，从而获得更精确的初始化值，大幅减少前向和反向传播中计算τ所需的迭代次数。同时设计了稀疏感知的GPU实现，以较低开销跳过零块。

Result: 在区块稀疏度为中高度（如>60%，常见于长上下文场景）时，AdaSplash-2的单步训练时间可匹配或优于FlashAttention-2。下游任务中，短上下文下模型性能与softmax基线相当，长上下文下则获得显著提升。

Conclusion: AdaSplash-2通过降低α-entmax attention的计算开销，使其在长上下文场景中成为高效且性能优异的稀疏注意力方案，为解决Transformer长序列训练的计算瓶颈提供了新思路。

Abstract: Sparse attention has been proposed as a way to alleviate the quadratic cost of transformers, a central bottleneck in long-context training. A promising line of work is $α$-entmax attention, a differentiable sparse alternative to softmax that enables input-dependent sparsity yet has lagged behind softmax due to the computational overhead necessary to compute the normalizer $τ$. In this paper, we introduce AdaSplash-2, which addresses this limitation through a novel histogram-based initialization that reduces the number of iterations needed to compute $τ$ to typically 1--2. The key idea is to compute a coarse histogram of attention scores on the fly and store it in on-chip SRAM, yielding a more accurate initialization that enables fast forward and backward computation. Combined with a sparsity-aware GPU implementation that skips zero blocks with low overhead, AdaSplash-2 matches or improves per-step training time relative to FlashAttention-2 when block sparsity is moderate-to-high (e.g., $>$60\%), which often occurs at long-context lengths. On downstream tasks, models trained with our efficient $α$-entmax attention match softmax baselines at short-context lengths and achieve substantial gains in long-context settings.

</details>


### [223] [One-shot learning for the complex dynamical behaviors of weakly nonlinear forced oscillators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15181)
*Teng Ma,Luca Rosafalco,Wei Cui,Lin Zhao,Attilio Frangi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种名为MEv-SINDy的单次学习方法，通过学习控制方程从单次激励时间序列中识别全局频率响应曲线。该方法结合广义谐波平衡（GHB）方法，将复杂受迫响应分解为慢变演化方程，成功应用于MEMS非线性梁谐振器和MEMS微镜，验证了其在宽激励范围内预测软化/硬化效应和跳跃现象的能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 复杂非线性动力系统的外推预测是工程领域的核心挑战，传统方法需要大量实验数据来表征系统频率响应曲线，数据获取负担沉重，亟需降低数据采集成本的高效识别方法。

Method: 提出MEv-SINDy方法，利用广义谐波平衡（GHB）方法将非自治多频系统中的复杂受迫响应分解为一系列慢变演化方程，然后通过稀疏识别技术从单次激励时间序列中推断系统控制方程，实现一次性学习。

Result: 在MEMS非线性梁谐振器和MEMS微镜两个关键微机电系统上验证了该方法的有效性。基于单点数据训练的模型能够准确预测系统在宽激励范围内的软化/硬化效应及跳跃现象。

Conclusion: MEv-SINDy方法显著降低了非线性微系统表征与设计的数据获取负担，为微机电系统的快速建模与优化设计提供了高效工具。

Abstract: Extrapolative prediction of complex nonlinear dynamics remains a central challenge in engineering. This study proposes a one-shot learning method to identify global frequency-response curves from a single excitation time history by learning governing equations. We introduce MEv-SINDy (Multi-frequency Evolutionary Sparse Identification of Nonlinear Dynamics) to infer the governing equations of non-autonomous and multi-frequency systems. The methodology leverages the Generalized Harmonic Balance (GHB) method to decompose complex forced responses into a set of slow-varying evolution equations. We validated the capabilities of MEv-SINDy on two critical Micro-Electro-Mechanical Systems (MEMS). These applications include a nonlinear beam resonator and a MEMS micromirror. Our results show that the model trained on a single point accurately predicts softening/hardening effects and jump phenomena across a wide range of excitation levels. This approach significantly reduces the data acquisition burden for the characterization and design of nonlinear microsystems.

</details>


### [224] [RL-STPA: Adapting System-Theoretic Hazard Analysis for Safety-Critical Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15201)
*Steven A. Senczyszyn,Timothy C. Havens,Nathaniel Rice,Jason E. Summers,Benjamin D. Werner,Benjamin J. Schumeg*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: As reinforcement learning (RL) deployments expand into safety-critical domains, existing evaluation methods fail to systematically identify hazards arising from the black-box nature of neural network enabled policies and distributional shift between training and deployment. This paper introduces Reinforcement Learning System-Theoretic Process Analysis (RL-STPA), a framework that adapts conventional STPA's systematic hazard analysis to address RL's unique challenges through three key contributions: hierarchical subtask decomposition using both temporal phase analysis and domain expertise to capture emergent behaviors, coverage-guided perturbation testing that explores the sensitivity of state-action spaces, and iterative checkpoints that feed identified hazards back into training through reward shaping and curriculum design. We demonstrate RL-STPA in the safety-critical test case of autonomous drone navigation and landing, revealing potential loss scenarios that can be missed by standard RL evaluations. The proposed framework provides practitioners with a toolkit for systematic hazard analysis, quantitative metrics for safety coverage assessment, and actionable guidelines for establishing operational safety bounds. While RL-STPA cannot provide formal guarantees for arbitrary neural policies, it offers a practical methodology for systematically evaluating and improving RL safety and robustness in safety-critical applications where exhaustive verification methods remain intractable.

</details>


### [225] [Optimal last-iterate convergence in matrix games with bandit feedback using the log-barrier](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15242)
*Come Fiegel,Pierre Menard,Tadashi Kozuno,Michal Valko,Vianney Perchet*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文研究零和矩阵博弈中的极小极大策略学习问题。针对解耦玩家场景下达到Ω(t^(-1/4))收敛下界的难题，提出使用对数障碍正则化结合双向分析的方法，成功实现了̃O(t^(-1/4))的高概率收敛，并将该方法推广到扩展形式博弈中。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Fiegel et al. (2025)已证明在玩家解耦的情况下，零和矩阵博弈中实现最后迭代收敛是一个困难的问题，其 exploitability gap 的下界为Ω(t^(-1/4))。虽然文献中已提出一些在线镜像下降算法，但尚未有算法真正达到该收敛速率。因此，需要设计新算法来填补这一理论与实践的差距。

Method: 核心方法包含两个关键技术：(1) 对数障碍(log-barrier)正则化技术；(2) 双向(dual-focused)分析框架。通过将这两种技术结合，设计了新的在线镜像下降算法。

Result: 证明了算法能够以高概率实现̃O(t^(-1/4))的收敛速率，达到了Fiegel et al.所证明的下界，从而在理论上证明了该方法的优越性。

Conclusion: 不仅在零和矩阵博弈中取得了最优收敛速率，更重要的是将这一技术成功扩展到扩展形式博弈(extensive-form games)，证明了相同的收敛速率，从而大幅拓展了方法的应用范围和理论保证。

Abstract: We study the problem of learning minimax policies in zero-sum matrix games. Fiegel et al. (2025) recently showed that achieving last-iterate convergence in this setting is harder when the players are uncoupled, by proving a lower bound on the exploitability gap of Omega(t^{-1/4}). Some online mirror descent algorithms were proposed in the literature for this problem, but none have truly attained this rate yet. We show that the use of a log-barrier regularization, along with a dual-focused analysis, allows this O-tilde(t^{-1/4}) convergence with high-probability. We additionally extend our idea to the setting of extensive-form games, proving a bound with the same rate.

</details>


### [226] [How Embeddings Shape Graph Neural Networks: Classical vs Quantum-Oriented Node Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15273)
*Nouhaila Innan,Antonello Rosato,Alberto Marchisio,Muhammad Shafique*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: Node embeddings act as the information interface for graph neural networks, yet their empirical impact is often reported under mismatched backbones, splits, and training budgets. This paper provides a controlled benchmark of embedding choices for graph classification, comparing classical baselines with quantum-oriented node representations under a unified pipeline. We evaluate two classical baselines alongside quantum-oriented alternatives, including a circuit-defined variational embedding and quantum-inspired embeddings computed via graph operators and linear-algebraic constructions. All variants are trained and tested with the same backbone, stratified splits, identical optimization and early stopping, and consistent metrics. Experiments on five different TU datasets and on QM9 converted to classification via target binning show clear dataset dependence: quantum-oriented embeddings yield the most consistent gains on structure-driven benchmarks, while social graphs with limited node attributes remain well served by classical baselines. The study highlights practical trade-offs between inductive bias, trainability, and stability under a fixed training budget, and offers a reproducible reference point for selecting quantum-oriented embeddings in graph learning.

</details>


### [227] [Benchmarking Optimizers for MLPs in Tabular Deep Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15297)
*Yury Gorishniy,Ivan Rubachev,Dmitrii Feoktistov,Artem Babenko*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 系统基准测试了多种优化器在表格数据监督学习任务上训练MLP模型的表现，发现Muon优化器一致优于AdamW，且权重指数移动平均(EMA)可提升AdamW在 vanilla MLP上的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习架构设计已广泛研究，但表格数据深度学习的优化器选择缺乏系统性基准测试，尽管新优化器在其他领域表现出潜力。

Method: 在多个表格数据集上，使用统一实验协议对MLP模型基准测试多种优化器。

Result: Muon优化器一致优于AdamW；EMA可提升AdamW在vanilla MLP上的表现，但对模型变体的效果不一致。

Conclusion: Muon应被视为表格深度学习的实用优化选择（若训练效率开销可接受）；EMA是简单有效的改进技术。

Abstract: MLP is a heavily used backbone in modern deep learning (DL) architectures for supervised learning on tabular data, and AdamW is the go-to optimizer used to train tabular DL models. Unlike architecture design, however, the choice of optimizer for tabular DL has not been examined systematically, despite new optimizers showing promise in other domains. To fill this gap, we benchmark \Noptimizers optimizers on \Ndatasets tabular datasets for training MLP-based models in the standard supervised learning setting under a shared experiment protocol. Our main finding is that the Muon optimizer consistently outperforms AdamW, and thus should be considered a strong and practical choice for practitioners and researchers, if the associated training efficiency overhead is affordable. Additionally, we find exponential moving average of model weights to be a simple yet effective technique that improves AdamW on vanilla MLPs, though its effect is less consistent across model variants.

</details>

### [228] [Knowledge Graph RAG: Agentic Crawling and Graph Construction in Enterprise Documents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14220)
*Koushik Chakraborty,Koyel Guha*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: Research proposes Agentic Knowledge Graphs with Recursive Crawling to enhance RAG systems for complex regulatory queries, achieving 70% accuracy improvement over standard vector-based approaches.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Traditional RAG pipelines fail to capture hierarchical and interconnected information in complex enterprise document ecosystems, leading to retrieval inaccuracies.

Method: Agentic Knowledge Graphs featuring Recursive Crawling to navigate superseding logic and multi-hop references in complex document hierarchies.

Result: Achieves a 70% accuracy improvement over standard vector-based RAG systems on the Code of Federal Regulations benchmark.

Conclusion: The Knowledge Graph-enhanced approach provides exhaustive and precise answers for complex regulatory queries, effectively solving the superseding logic and multi-hop reference challenges in enterprise document ecosystems.

Abstract: This research paper addresses the limitations of semantic search in complex enterprise document ecosystems. Traditional RAG pipelines often fail to capture hierarchical and interconnected information, leading to retrieval inaccuracies. We propose Agentic Knowledge Graphs featuring Recursive Crawling as a robust solution for navigating superseding logic and multi-hop references. Our benchmark evaluation using the Code of Federal Regulations (CFR) demonstrates that this Knowledge Graph-enhanced approach achieves a 70% accuracy improvement over standard vector-based RAG systems, providing exhaustive and precise answers for complex regulatory queries.

</details>


### [229] [Adaptive Query Routing: A Tier-Based Framework for Hybrid Retrieval Across Financial, Legal, and Medical Documents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14222)
*Afshan Hashmi*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文对比了三种检索增强生成（RAG）架构——向量RAG、树推理和自适应混合检索（AHR），在金融、法律、医学领域的问答任务中的表现。树推理总体得分最高（0.900），但不同复杂度的查询任务存在差异化优势：向量RAG擅长多文档合成，树推理和混合AHR在跨引用任务中召回率达100%。在真实财务基准FinanceBench验证中，树推理以0.938领先混合AHR的0.901和向量RAG的0.821，差距达11.7个百分点。研究表明应根据查询复杂度和文档结构动态选择检索策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前RAG系统在处理不同复杂度查询时缺乏自适应能力，已有评估主要关注特定领域或单一范式，尚无系统比较三种主流检索架构在不同查询层级和领域的表现差异。

Method: 在金融、法律、医学三个领域实现三种检索架构（向量RAG、树推理、自适应混合检索），引入四级查询复杂度基准，使用GPT-4作为评判模型进行评估，并在包含150个真实SEC文件标注问题的FinanceBench上进行验证。

Result: 树推理总体最高（0.900），但无单一范式通吃：Tier 4多文档合成中向量RAG胜出（0.900），跨引用任务混合AHR最优（0.850），多段落查询AHR最佳（0.929）。树/混合方法跨引用召回率100% vs 向量搜索91.7%。FinanceBench验证：树推理0.938，混合AHR 0.901，向量RAG 0.821，真实文档差距扩大至11.7%。

Conclusion: 不存在普适最优检索范式，应开发自适应检索系统，根据查询复杂度和文档结构动态选择策略。树推理在整体精度和跨引用能力上优势显著，向量RAG在多文档综合场景仍有竞争力。

Abstract: Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) has become the standard paradigm for grounding Large Language Model outputs in external knowledge. Lumer et al. [1] presented the first systematic evaluation comparing vector-based agentic RAG against hierarchical node-based reasoning systems for financial document QA across 1,200 SEC filings, finding vector-based systems achieved a 68% win rate. Concurrently, the PageIndex framework [2] demonstrated 98.7% accuracy on FinanceBench through purely reasoning-based retrieval. This paper extends their work by: (i) implementing and evaluating three retrieval architectures: Vector RAG, Tree Reasoning, and the proposed Adaptive Hybrid Retrieval (AHR) across financial, legal, and medical domains; (ii) introducing a four-tier query complexity benchmark; and (iii) employing GPT-4-powered LLM-as-judge evaluation. Experiments reveal that Tree Reasoning achieves the highest overall score (0.900), but no single paradigm dominates across all tiers: Vector RAG wins on multi-document synthesis (Tier 4, score 0.900), while the Hybrid AHR achieves the best performance on cross-reference (0.850) and multi-section queries (0.929). Cross-reference recall reaches 100% for tree-based and hybrid approaches versus 91.7% for vector search, quantifying a critical capability gap. Validation on FinanceBench (150 expert-annotated questions on real SEC 10-K and 10-Q filings) confirms and strengthens these findings: Tree Reasoning scores 0.938, Hybrid AHR 0.901, and Vector RAG 0.821, with the Tree--Vector quality gap widening to 11.7 percentage points on real-world documents. These findings support the development of adaptive retrieval systems that dynamically select strategies based on query complexity and document structure. All code and data are publicly available.

</details>


### [230] [TRACE: A Conversational Framework for Sustainable Tourism Recommendation with Agentic Counterfactual Explanations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14223)
*Ashmi Banerjee,Adithi Satish,Wolfgang Wörndl,Yashar Deldjoo*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: TRACE是一个基于多智能体LLM的可持续旅游推荐框架，通过智能体反事实解释和LLM澄清问题引导用户反思，在不强制干预的情况下推荐更环保的替代方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统对话式旅游推荐系统过度优化用户相关性和便利性，导致热门目的地过度拥挤、碳密集型旅行选择增多的问题。

Method: 采用模块化编排器-工作者架构，包含专门化智能体用于：1) 挖掘潜在可持续性偏好；2) 构建结构化用户画像；3) 生成兼顾相关性与环境影响的推荐。核心创新包括智能体反事实解释和LLM驱动的澄清问题。

Result: 用户研究和语义对齐分析表明，TRACE有效支持可持续决策制定，同时保持推荐质量和交互响应性。

Conclusion: TRACE通过多智能体架构和反事实解释技术，在保持推荐质量和交互响应性的同时，有效支持用户做出更环保的旅游决策。系统在Google ADK上实现，代码和演示均已开源公开。

Abstract: Traditional conversational travel recommender systems primarily optimize for user relevance and convenience, often reinforcing popular, overcrowded destinations and carbon-intensive travel choices. To address this, we present TRACE (Tourism Recommendation with Agentic Counterfactual Explanations), a multi-agent, LLM-based framework that promotes sustainable tourism through interactive nudging. TRACE uses a modular orchestrator-worker architecture where specialized agents elicit latent sustainability preferences, construct structured user personas, and generate recommendations that balance relevance with environmental impact.
  A key innovation lies in its use of agentic counterfactual explanations and LLM-driven clarifying questions, which together surface greener alternatives and refine understanding of intent, fostering user reflection without coercion. User studies and semantic alignment analyses demonstrate that TRACE effectively supports sustainable decision-making while preserving recommendation quality and interactive responsiveness. TRACE is implemented on Google's Agent Development Kit, with full code, Docker setup, prompts, and a publicly available demo video to ensure reproducibility.
  A project summary, including all resources, prompts, and demo access, is available at https://ashmibanerjee.github.io/trace-chatbot.

</details>


### [231] [FRESCO: Benchmarking and Optimizing Re-rankers for Evolving Semantic Conflict in Retrieval-Augmented Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14227)
*Sohyun An,Hayeon Lee,Shuibenyang Yuan,Chun-cheng Jason Chen,Cho-Jui Hsieh,Vijai Mohan,Alexander Min*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: FRESCO是一个用于评估重排序器在时间动态环境中的基准测试。研究发现现有重排序器存在偏向于语义丰富但事实过时的旧文档的偏差，通过指令优化可获得高达27%的提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RAG是缓解LLM时间陈旧性的关键方法，重排序器在其中起关键作用。但现有基准测试主要评估静态设置下的重排序器，未能充分评估动态信息演变场景下的性能——这是一个关键缺口，因为现实系统常需在不同时间戳的证据中做出选择。

Method: 提出FRESCO基准，通过将时效性查询与历史Wikipedia修订版本配对，测试重排序器能否在保持语义相关性的同时优先选择事实新鲜的证据。进一步研究指令优化框架，通过识别平衡"演变知识"和"非演变知识"任务的Pareto最优指令来缓解偏旧问题。

Result: 现有重排序器存在一致的失效模式：对语义丰富但事实过时的旧文档有强烈偏好，即使这些文档已过时。指令优化后，在演变知识任务上获得高达27%的提升，同时在非演变知识任务上保持竞争力。

Conclusion: 通过指令优化可有效缓解重排序器对旧文档的偏好问题；识别Pareto最优指令能够在冲突任务需求间取得平衡，为RAG系统在动态信息环境中提供更优性能。

Abstract: Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) is a key approach to mitigating the temporal staleness of large language models (LLMs) by grounding responses in up-to-date evidence. Within the RAG pipeline, re-rankers play a pivotal role in selecting the most useful documents from retrieved candidates. However, existing benchmarks predominantly evaluate re-rankers in static settings and do not adequately assess performance under evolving information -- a critical gap, as real-world systems often must choose among temporally different pieces of evidence. To address this limitation, we introduce FRESCO (Factual Recency and Evolving Semantic COnflict), a benchmark for evaluating re-rankers in temporally dynamic contexts. By pairing recency-seeking queries with historical Wikipedia revisions, FRESCO tests whether re-rankers can prioritize factually recent evidence while maintaining semantic relevance. Our evaluation reveals a consistent failure mode across existing re-rankers: a strong bias toward older, semantically rich documents, even when they are factually obsolete. We further investigate an instruction optimization framework to mitigate this issue. By identifying Pareto-optimal instructions that balance Evolving and Non-Evolving Knowledge tasks, we obtain gains of up to 27% on Evolving Knowledge tasks while maintaining competitive performance on Non-Evolving Knowledge tasks.

</details>


### [232] [Controlling Authority Retrieval: A Missing Retrieval Objective for Authority-Governed Knowledge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14488)
*Andre Bacellar*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 论文首次形式化定义"控制权威检索"(CAR)问题，研究如何从文档集合中精确检索当前有效的权威文档而非已被替代的过时文档。理论部分证明检索正确性的充要条件并建立最优Scope识别上界；实证部分在安全公告、最高法院判例和FDA药品记录三个真实数据集上验证Two-Stage检索架构的显著优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在法律、药品监管、软件安全等领域，权威文档的更替往往导致语义相关性高的旧文档"失效"但仍被检索返回。传统Dense RAG方法无法区分文档的权威状态，造成"已废弃但高相关"文档持续占据检索结果前列，导致下游系统产生错误决策。

Method: 提出TCA(R,q)形式化框架，将CAR问题定义为恢复权威闭包的前沿集合；定理4给出检索结果正确性的充要条件（前沿包含+无忽略替代）；命题2通过对抗置换论证建立最优Scope识别上界；采用Dense检索与Two-Stage（先Dense后权威验证）对比实验范式。

Result: Dense检索在三大数据集上表现有限（FDA=0.064, SCOTUS=0.172, Security=0.270），Two-Stage方法显著优于基线（Security@5从0.270→0.975, SCOTUS从0.172→0.926, FDA从0.064→0.774）。GPT-4o-mini实验显示Dense RAG对39%的查询产生"未修补"错误陈述，Two-Stage降至16%。

Conclusion: 研究通过形式化定义CAR问题，建立了检索理论与权威文档管理之间的桥梁，并提供了可验证的理论框架。三种真实世界数据集验证了理论结构的有效性，证明了Two-Stage方法在权威文档检索中的必要性。开源的基准数据集和评估工具为后续研究提供了可复现的实验基础。

Abstract: In any domain where knowledge accumulates under formal authority -- law, drug regulation, software security -- a later document can formally void an earlier one while remaining semantically distant from it. We formalize this as Controlling Authority Retrieval (CAR): recovering the active frontier front(cl(A_k(q))) of the authority closure of the semantic anchor set -- a different mathematical problem from argmax_d s(q,d). The two central results are: Theorem 4 (CAR-Correctness Characterization) gives necessary-and-sufficient conditions on any retrieved set R for TCA(R,q)=1 -- frontier inclusion and no-ignored-superseder -- independent of how R was produced. Proposition 2 (Scope Identifiability Upper Bound) establishes phi(q) as a hard worst-case ceiling: for any scope-indexed algorithm, TCA@k <= phi(q) * R_anchor(q), proved by an adversarial permutation argument. Three independent real-world corpora validate the proved structure: security advisories (Dense TCA@5=0.270, two-stage 0.975), SCOTUS overruling pairs (Dense=0.172, two-stage 0.926), FDA drug records (Dense=0.064, two-stage 0.774). A GPT-4o-mini experiment shows the downstream cost: Dense RAG produces explicit "not patched" claims for 39% of queries where a patch exists; Two-Stage cuts this to 16%. Four benchmark datasets, domain adapters, and a single-command scorer are released at https://github.com/andremir/car-retrieval.

</details>


### [233] [Category-based and Popularity-guided Video Game Recommendation: A Balance-oriented Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14598)
*Xiping Li,Jianghong Ma,Kangzhe Liu,Shanshan Feng,Haijun Zhang,Yutong Wang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出CPGRec框架，通过三个模块（准确性模块、多样性模块和综合模块）解决视频游戏推荐系统中准确性-多样性权衡的问题。实验在Steam数据集上验证了方法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前视频游戏推荐系统存在三个主要问题：1）过度重视准确性而忽视多样性，导致推荐结果同质化；2）游戏之间的关联建模不够紧密，影响推荐准确性；3）现有多样性方法未充分利用物品类别和流行度等关键信息进行邻居建模和消息传播。

Method: 提出CPGRec框架，包含三个模块：1）准确性驱动模块：通过更严格的方式连接游戏，增强推荐准确性；2）多样性驱动模块：在游戏图中连接不同类别的邻居，利用热门游戏节点放大长尾游戏的影响；3）综合模块：结合前两个模块，采用负样本评分重加权方法平衡准确性与多样性。

Result: 在Steam数据集上的实验结果表明，CPGRec能够有效提升游戏推荐效果，在准确性与多样性之间取得更好的平衡。

Conclusion: 本文提出的CPGRec框架通过多模块设计和负样本评分重加权策略，成功解决了视频游戏推荐中的准确性-多样性权衡问题，为游戏推荐系统提供了新的解决方案。

Abstract: In recent years, the video game industry has experienced substantial growth, presenting players with a vast array of game choices. This surge in options has spurred the need for a specialized recommender system tailored for video games. However, current video game recommendation approaches tend to prioritize accuracy over diversity, potentially leading to unvaried game suggestions. In addition, the existing game recommendation methods commonly lack the ability to establish strict connections between games to enhance accuracy. Furthermore, many existing diversity-focused methods fail to leverage crucial item information, such as item category and popularity during neighbor modeling and message propagation. To address these challenges, we introduce a novel framework, called CPGRec, comprising three modules, namely accuracy-driven, diversity-driven, and comprehensive modules. The first module extends the state-of-the-art accuracy-focused game recommendation method by connecting games in a more stringent manner to enhance recommendation accuracy. The second module connects neighbors with diverse categories within the proposed game graph and harnesses the advantages of popular game nodes to amplify the influence of long-tail games within the player-game bipartite graph, thereby enriching recommendation diversity. The third module combines the above two modules and employs a new negative-sample rating score reweighting method to balance accuracy and diversity. Experimental results on the Steam dataset demonstrate the effectiveness of our proposed method in improving game recommendations. The dataset and source codes are anonymously released at: https://github.com/CPGRec2024/CPGRec.git.

</details>


### [234] [Uncertainty-aware Generative Learning Path Recommendation with Cognition-Adaptive Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14613)
*Xiangrui Xiong,Hang Liang,Baiyang Chen,Zifei Pan,Yanli Lee*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: U-GLAD proposes an uncertainty-aware generative approach for learning path recommendation, using Gaussian LSTM to model cognitive uncertainty, goal-oriented concept encoder with multi-head attention for personalization, and diffusion model for generative prediction of optimal learning concepts.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Current LPR methods fail to account for historical interaction uncertainty (e.g., lucky guesses or accidental slips) and lack adaptability to diverse learning goals.

Method: Gaussian LSTM models cognitive states as probability distributions; goal-oriented concept encoder uses multi-head attention and objective-specific transformations; generative diffusion model predicts latent representation of next optimal concept.

Result: Significantly outperforms representative baselines on three public datasets.

Conclusion: U-GLAD significantly outperforms representative baselines on three public datasets, with superior capability in perceiving interaction uncertainty and providing stable, goal-driven recommendation paths.

Abstract: Learning Path Recommendation (LPR) is critical for personalized education, yet current methods often fail to account for historical interaction uncertainty (e.g., lucky guesses or accidental slips) and lack adaptability to diverse learning goals. We propose U-GLAD (Uncertainty-aware Generative Learning Path Recommendation with Cognition-Adaptive Diffusion). To address representation bias, the framework models cognitive states as probability distributions, capturing the learner's underlying true state via a Gaussian LSTM. To ensure highly personalized recommendation, a goal-oriented concept encoder utilizes multi-head attention and objective-specific transformations to dynamically align concept semantics with individual learning goals, generating uniquely tailored embeddings. Unlike traditional discriminative ranking approaches, our model employs a generative diffusion model to predict the latent representation of the next optimal concept. Extensive evaluations on three public datasets demonstrate that U-GLAD significantly outperforms representative baselines. Further analyses confirm its superior capability in perceiving interaction uncertainty and providing stable, goal-driven recommendation paths.

</details>


### [235] [Federated User Behavior Modeling for Privacy-Preserving LLM Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14833)
*Lei Guo,Hongyun Yang,Pengjie Ren,Tong Chen,Hui Liu,Zhumin Chen*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出SF-UBM，一种基于语义增强的联邦用户行为建模方法，用于解决隐私保护跨域推荐(PPCDR)中的三大挑战：非重叠域的身份对齐、跨域数据模态异构性、以及协同过滤信号与LLM特征空间的融合。方法通过语义桥接联邦架构、事实反驳知识蒸馏和软提示空间对齐三大核心模块，在三个真实领域数据集上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM推荐系统受限于用户数据的有限性和稀疏性。跨域推荐虽可缓解此问题，但现有方法假设域可重叠访问，无法解决隐私保护场景。隐私保护跨域推荐(Non-overlapping PPCDR)的三大挑战包括：(1)用户身份/行为数据不可跨域共享，阻碍跨域对齐；(2)跨域数据模态异构性增加知识整合难度；(3)传统协同过滤信号与LLM特征空间差异大，难以融合。

Method: 提出SF-UBM (Semantic-enhanced Federated User Behavior Modeling)方法，包含三个核心模块：1) 语义增强联邦架构：利用自然语言作为通用桥梁连接非重叠域，文本型物品表征加密共享，用户数据本地存储；2) 事实反驳知识蒸馏(Fact-counter Knowledge Distillation)：整合领域无关知识与领域特定知识，跨越不同数据模态；3) 软提示空间对齐：将预学习用户偏好和跨域物品表征投影到软提示空间，对齐行为空间与语义空间。

Result: 在三个真实领域数据集上进行广泛实验，结果表明SF-UBM相比近期SOTA方法具有更优的有效性。

Conclusion: SF-UBM通过语义增强的联邦架构有效解决了隐私保护跨域推荐中的域对齐难题；事实反驳知识蒸馏模块成功融合了跨模态知识；软提示空间对齐机制实现了协同过滤信号与LLM的有效整合。该工作为LLM时代的隐私保护推荐系统提供了新思路。

Abstract: Large Language Models have shown great success in recommender systems. However, the limited and sparse nature of user data often restricts the LLM's ability to effectively model behavior patterns. To address this, existing studies have explored cross-domain solutions by conducting Cross-Domain Recommendation tasks. But previous methods typically assume domains are overlapped and can be accessed readily. None of the LLM methods address the privacy-preserving issues in the CDR settings, that is, Privacy-Preserving Cross-Domain Recommendation. Conducting non-overlapping PPCDR with LLM is challenging since: 1)The inability to share user identity or behavioral data across domains impedes effective cross-domain alignment. 2)The heterogeneity of data modalities across domains complicates knowledge integration. 3)Fusing collaborative filtering signals from traditional recommendation models with LLMs is difficult, as they operate within distinct feature spaces. To address the above issues, we propose SF-UBM, a Semantic-enhanced Federated User Behavior Modeling method. Specifically, to deal with Challenge 1, we leverage natural language as a universal bridge to connect disjoint domains via a semantic-enhanced federated architecture. Here, text-based item representations are encrypted and shared, while user-specific data remains local. To handle Challenge 2, we design a Fact-counter Knowledge Distillation module to integrate domain-agnostic knowledge with domain-specific knowledge, across different data modalities. To tackle Challenge 3, we project pre-learned user preferences and cross-domain item representations into the soft prompt space, aligning behavioral and semantic spaces for effective LLM learning. We conduct extensive experiments on three pairs of real-world domains, and the experimental results demonstrate the effectiveness of SF-UBM compared to the recent SOTA methods.

</details>


### [236] [Well Begun is Half Done: Training-Free and Model-Agnostic Semantically Guaranteed User Representation Initialization for Multimodal Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14839)
*Jinfeng Xu,Zheyu Chen,Shuo Yang,Jinze Li,Hewei Wang,Jianheng Tang,Wei Wang,Xiping Hu,Edith C. H. Ngai*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出SG-URInit方法，通过整合用户交互物品的模态特征和聚类的全局特征，为用户构建语义增强的初始表示，解决多模态推荐中用户与物品因初始化方式不同导致的语义鸿沟问题。该方法无需训练且模型无关，可直接集成到现有多模态推荐模型中。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法忽略了用户表示初始化的关键作用。由于用户本身缺乏固有模态信息，其随机初始化的表示与基于丰富模态信息初始化的物品表示之间存在显著的语义鸿沟。

Method: SG-URInit通过整合两个层面的特征来构建用户初始表示：(1) 用户交互物品的模态特征（局部/物品级语义）；(2) 对应聚类的全局特征（全局/聚类级语义）。该方法具有训练无关(training-free)和模型无关(model-agnostic)的特性。

Result: 实验结果表明，将SG-URInit集成到先进的多模态推荐模型中可显著提升推荐性能，同时有效缓解物品冷启动问题并加速模型收敛。

Conclusion: SG-URInit是一种高效、实用的多模态推荐解决方案，无需额外训练开销即可无缝集成到现有模型中，同时兼顾局部和全局语义信息的捕获。

Abstract: Recent advancements in multimodal recommendations, which leverage diverse modality information to mitigate data sparsity and improve recommendation accuracy, have gained significant attention. However, existing multimodal recommendations overlook the critical role of user representation initialization. Unlike items, which are naturally associated with rich modality information, users lack such inherent information. Consequently, item representations initialized based on meaningful modality information and user representations initialized randomly exhibit a significant semantic gap.
  To this end, we propose a Semantically Guaranteed User Representation Initialization (SG-URInit). SG-URInit constructs the initial representation for each user by integrating both the modality features of the items they have interacted with and the global features of their corresponding clusters. SG-URInit enables the initialization of semantically enriched user representations that effectively capture both local (item-level) and global (cluster-level) semantics.
  Our SG-URInit is training-free and model-agnostic, meaning it can be seamlessly integrated into existing multimodal recommendation models without incurring any additional computational overhead during training. Extensive experiments on multiple real-world datasets demonstrate that incorporating SG-URInit into advanced multimodal recommendation models significantly enhances recommendation performance. Furthermore, the results show that SG-URInit can further alleviate the item cold-start problem and also accelerate model convergence, making it an efficient and practical solution for multimodal recommendations.

</details>


### [237] [GenRec: A Preference-Oriented Generative Framework for Large-Scale Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14878)
*Yanyan Zou,Junbo Qi,Lunsong Huang,Yu Li,Kewei Xu,Jiabao Gao,Binglei Zhao,Xuanhua Yang,Sulong Xu,Shengjie Li*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出GenRec框架，解决工业级生成式检索(GR)在推荐系统中规模化部署的三大挑战：分页请求导致的不一致输出、长序列多token编码的高成本、以及生成策略与用户偏好的对齐问题。该方案在JD App完成部署，在线A/B测试中点击率提升9.5%，交易量提升8.7%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式检索(GR)通过下一token预测(NTP)范式为推荐系统带来新思路，但在工业级大规模部署时面临严峻挑战：(1)分页请求机制导致相同模型输入产生不一致输出；(2)基于语义ID的多token item表示导致长用户行为序列编码成本过高；(3)生成策略难以精准对齐用户细微偏好信号。这些挑战阻碍了GR从学术研究向生产系统的落地。

Method: GenRec采用单一decoder-only架构，通过三方面技术创新解决上述挑战：①训练目标：提出Page-wise NTP任务，对整个交互页面进行监督而非单个item，提供更密集的梯度信号，解决逐点训练的一对多歧义问题；②预填充阶段：设计非对称线性Token Merger模块，在保持完整分辨率解码的同时压缩多token语义ID的提示长度，输入长度降低约2倍且精度损失可忽略；③偏好对齐：引入GRPO-SR强化学习方法，结合Group Relative Policy Optimization与NLL正则化保证训练稳定性，采用Hybrid Rewards（密集奖励模型+相关性门控）缓解奖励黑客问题。

Result: 在JD App生产环境中进行为期一个月的在线A/B测试，GenRec相较于现有推荐管线实现：点击量提升9.5%，交易量提升8.7%，验证了该框架在实际工业场景中的有效性和可行性。

Conclusion: GenRec成功将生成式检索技术应用于超大规模电商推荐系统，通过Page-wise NTP、Token Merger压缩和GRPO-SR偏好对齐三大创新点，有效解决了工业部署中的输出不一致、计算成本和偏好对齐难题，为生成式推荐系统的工业化落地提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Generative Retrieval (GR) offers a promising paradigm for recommendation through next-token prediction (NTP). However, scaling it to large-scale industrial systems introduces three challenges: (i) within a single request, the identical model inputs may produce inconsistent outputs due to the pagination request mechanism; (ii) the prohibitive cost of encoding long user behavior sequences with multi-token item representations based on semantic IDs, and (iii) aligning the generative policy with nuanced user preference signals. We present GenRec, a preference-oriented generative framework deployed on the JD App that addresses above challenges within a single decoder-only architecture. For training objective, we propose Page-wise NTP task, which supervises over an entire interaction page rather than each interacted item individually, providing denser gradient signal and resolving the one-to-many ambiguity of point-wise training. On the prefilling side, an asymmetric linear Token Merger compresses multi-token Semantic IDs in the prompt while preserving full-resolution decoding, reducing input length by ~2X with negligible accuracy loss. To further align outputs with user satisfaction, we introduce GRPO-SR, a reinforcement learning method that pairs Group Relative Policy Optimization with NLL regularization for training stability, and employs Hybrid Rewards combining a dense reward model with a relevance gate to mitigate reward hacking. In month-long online A/B tests serving production traffic, GenRec achieves 9.5% improvement in click count and 8.7% in transaction count over the existing pipeline.

</details>


### [238] [SAGER: Self-Evolving User Policy Skills for Recommendation Agent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14972)
*Zhen Tao,Riwei Lai,Chenyun Yu,Weixin Chen,Li Chen,Beibei Kong,Lei Cheng,Chengxiang Zhuo,Zang Li,Qingqiang Sun*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: SAGER是首个为每个用户配备专用策略技能（policy skill）的推荐智能体框架，通过自然语言文档编码个性化决策原则，实现推理过程的持续进化，解决了传统LLM推荐系统中记忆个性化而推理逻辑静态化的根本矛盾。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前LLM推荐智能体存在结构性瓶颈：个性化仅体现在用户语义记忆层面，而推理逻辑仍依赖所有用户共享的静态系统提示。当推荐失败时，智能体仅更新用户偏好记忆，却无法审视导致失败的决策逻辑，导致推理过程在错误累积中保持结构不变。

Method: 1) 双表示技能架构：将丰富的进化基质与极简的推理时注入解耦；2) 增量对比思维链引擎：通过对比接受项与未选向项诊断推理缺陷，同时保留既有先验知识；3) 技能增强的列表级推理：在技能提供真正区分价值之处创建细粒度决策边界。

Result: 在四个公共基准数据集上的实验表明，SAGER达到最优性能，且性能提升与记忆积累正交，证实了个性化推理过程本身是推荐改进的独特来源。

Conclusion: 本文首次证明了个性化推理过程（而非仅个性化记忆）是提升LLM推荐系统性能的有效途径，为推荐系统领域开辟了新的研究方向。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) based recommendation agents personalize what they know through evolving per-user semantic memory, yet how they reason remains a universal, static system prompt shared identically across all users. This asymmetry is a fundamental bottleneck: when a recommendation fails, the agent updates its memory of user preferences but never interrogates the decision logic that produced the failure, leaving its reasoning process structurally unchanged regardless of how many mistakes it accumulates. To address this bottleneck, we propose SAGER (Self-Evolving Agent for Personalized Recommendation), the first recommendation agent framework in which each user is equipped with a dedicated policy skill, a structured natural-language document encoding personalized decision principles that evolves continuously through interaction. SAGER introduces a two-representation skill architecture that decouples a rich evolution substrate from a minimal inference-time injection, an incremental contrastive chain-of-thought engine that diagnoses reasoning flaws by contrasting accepted against unchosen items while preserving accumulated priors, and skill-augmented listwise reasoning that creates fine-grained decision boundaries where the evolved skill provides genuine discriminative value. Experiments on four public benchmarks demonstrate that SAGER achieves state-of-the-art performance, with gains orthogonal to memory accumulation, confirming that personalizing the reasoning process itself is a qualitatively distinct source of recommendation improvement.

</details>


### [239] [Metric-agnostic Learning-to-Rank via Boosting and Rank Approximation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15101)
*Camilo Gomez,Pengyang Wang,Yanjie Fu*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出一种新颖的列表式LTR框架，通过可微分排序损失函数结合平滑近似算子和梯度提升机，实现高效且可泛化的排序学习，有效解决了传统方法依赖单一不可微排名指标导致的训练不稳定和泛化能力有限的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前LTR方法依赖单一前缀排序指标（如NDCG或MAP）进行优化，存在两个局限：(1)非可微问题：直接优化排序指标本质上是不可平滑的，导致训练过程不稳定且低效；(2)排序效用有限：仅优化单一指标难以泛化到其他排序指标。

Method: (1)提出一种新的可微分排序损失函数，结合排序算子的平滑近似与每个查询的平均均方损失；(2)将梯度提升机适配到最小化所提损失函数，以列表为单位进行优化。

Result: 实验结果表明，该方法在信息检索指标上优于当前最先进的算法，同时保持相似的效率。

Conclusion: 该框架通过可微分损失函数和梯度提升机的结合，成功实现了高效且可泛化的排序学习，为解决LTR中单一指标优化的局限性提供了有效途径。

Abstract: Learning-to-Rank (LTR) is a supervised machine learning approach that constructs models specifically designed to order a set of items or documents based on their relevance or importance to a given query or context. Despite significant success in real-world information retrieval systems, current LTR methods rely on one prefix ranking metric (e.g., such as Normalized Discounted Cumulative Gain (NDCG) or Mean Average Precision (MAP)) for optimizing the ranking objective function. Such metric-dependent setting limits LTR methods from two perspectives: (1) non-differentiable problem: directly optimizing ranking functions over a given ranking metric is inherently non-smooth, making the training process unstable and inefficient; (2) limited ranking utility: optimizing over one single metric makes it difficult to generalize well to other ranking metrics of interest. To address the above issues, we propose a novel listwise LTR framework for efficient and generalizable ranking purpose. Specifically, we propose a new differentiable ranking loss that combines a smooth approximation to the ranking operator with the average mean square loss per query. Then, we adapt gradient-boosting machines to minimize our proposed loss with respect to each list, a novel contribution. Finally, extensive experimental results confirm that our method outperforms the current state-of-the-art in information retrieval measures with similar efficiency.

</details>

# cs.MA [[Back]](#toc)

### [240] [FedGUI: Benchmarking Federated GUI Agents across Heterogeneous Platforms, Devices, and Operating Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14956)
*Wenhao Wang,Haoting Shi,Mengying Yuan,Yiquan Lin,Panrong Tong,Hanzhang Zhou,Guangyi Liu,Pengxiang Zhao,Yue Wang,Siheng Chen*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文提出FedGUI，首个跨移动端、Web端和桌面平台开发及评估联邦GUI智能体的综合基准，通过六套数据集系统研究四种异构性维度，证实跨平台协作的优势，并识别平台和操作系统为最关键因素。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统集中式方法训练GUI智能体面临成本高和可扩展性受限的挑战；联邦学习虽具潜力，但缺乏能够捕获真实世界跨平台异构性的基准。

Method: 提出了FedGUI基准，包含六个精选数据集，系统研究四种异构性类型：跨平台、跨设备、跨操作系统和跨来源。

Result: 跨平台协作能够提升性能；平台和操作系统被识别为最具影响力的异构性因素。

Conclusion: FedGUI为社区提供了一个重要基础，用于构建更可扩展且保护隐私的GUI智能体，以实现实际部署。

Abstract: Training GUI agents with traditional centralized methods faces significant cost and scalability challenges. Federated learning (FL) offers a promising solution, yet its potential is hindered by the lack of benchmarks that capture real-world, cross-platform heterogeneity. To bridge this gap, we introduce FedGUI, the first comprehensive benchmark for developing and evaluating federated GUI agents across mobile, web, and desktop platforms. FedGUI provides a suite of six curated datasets to systematically study four crucial types of heterogeneity: cross-platform, cross-device, cross-OS, and cross-source. Extensive experiments reveal several key insights: First, we show that cross-platform collaboration improves performance, extending prior mobile-only federated learning to diverse GUI environments; Second, we demonstrate the presence of distinct heterogeneity dimensions and identify platform and OS as the most influential factors. FedGUI provides a vital foundation for the community to build more scalable and privacy-preserving GUI agents for real-world deployment. Our code and data are publicly available at https://github.com/wwh0411/FedGUI..

</details>
